- <a id="dec-ux-71"></a>**DEC-UX-71** · The Parameters of a query are a second box under the editor, which folds open and shut. A Parameter has a name, a kind from a standard list and a value; the value is a value or an SQL function, worked out at every run, not a separate default. A Parameter is made or changed in a floating window, one at a time. A Parameter that is not in the SQL is grey; an `@name` in the SQL that is not a Parameter stands out, and the query cannot run until it is defined. *Decided on 2026-10-27, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-72"></a>**DEC-UX-72** · The result of a query is a grid like that of BigQuery, in the style of Data Hub: a row number, a column per field with its type, columns made narrower or wider by the edge of their head, a row on one line, alternate rows lightly shaded, and 100 rows a page ([FR-SR-51](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#fr-sr-51)). Sorting from the head of a column sorts the page only. *Decided on 2026-10-27, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-73"></a>**DEC-UX-73** · A query is bounded by its Resources: a query is its name, its question and its Resources, and it reads only those. A table in its SQL that is not one of them is an error, and the query cannot run. Adding or removing a Resource starts a new query, which takes the SQL and the Parameters along and says where it started from; the old query stays as it was. Several queries may share the same Resources, each with its own question and history. While writing, "Lenses on these resources" shows the Lenses that already use them. *Decided on 2026-10-27, by Max, after a brainstorm on how to keep the runs of a query together.* *Note of 2026-11-13: a power user may also read tables Data Hub knows that are no Resource ([DEC-UX-220](#dec-ux-220)).* *Note of 2026-11-14: where it started from is said in its History, no longer under its question ([DEC-UX-250](#dec-ux-250)).* *Note of 2026-11-19: adding or taking out a Resource changes this query; a new query with the new set stays possible, no longer the required route ([DEC-UX-287](#dec-ux-287)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-74"></a>**DEC-UX-74** · Every run is a variation of its query, of its values, its SQL or both, and goes into its history. The panel of Query lists one item per query, not per run, with how many runs; the query that is open unfolds to its last runs. The previews of a Resource and a query of Claude are items of their own, each with its history; the runs of a Lens are in the history of the Lens. *Decided on 2026-10-27, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-75"></a>**DEC-UX-75** · Making a Lens shows no "+ Parameter", no choice whether Users may change the Parameters, and no step-by-step description: the Parameters come from the query, Users can always change them, and Data Hub writes what the Lens does when it is saved ([DEC-LE-01](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#dec-le-01), [DEC-LE-02](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#dec-le-02)). *Decided on 2026-10-27, by Max.* *Note of 2026-10-29: its author writes what it does, step by step ([DEC-UX-121](#dec-ux-121)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-76"></a>**DEC-UX-76** · The box of the SQL in Query folds open and shut, as the box of the Parameters does: folded, it is its head with the number of lines and of CTEs. *Decided on 2026-10-27, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-77"></a>**DEC-UX-77** · A query used as a Lens says so under its question, with a link to the Lens; in its head "Update the lens" takes the place of "Make it a lens", and "Lenses on these resources" is not shown. "Update the lens" opens a sheet: what it replaces and what stays, the size check, and the button ([FR-LE-16](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#fr-le-16)). *Decided on 2026-10-27, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-78"></a>**DEC-UX-78** · The background of the light theme is soft clouds of colour on white, in CSS: warm yellow and peach in the upper left, a soft rose, lilac in the middle and sky blue in the lower right, made from a picture Max gave, turned a quarter to lie wide and mirrored; no pink field. The dark theme keeps its gradient; a painted picture still takes the place of either ([DEC-UX-34](#dec-ux-34)). *Decided on 2026-10-27, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-79"></a>**DEC-UX-79** · A Message that Data Hub writes itself has a block of its own right under its text, "Understand what is happening here", holding the Learning Card that explains it, as a card is attached to a Message ([DEC-UX-41](#dec-ux-41)); never a marker or an inline link inside its text ([DEC-UX-42](#dec-ux-42)). A click opens the card inline, in the Message, not in a floating window. *Decided on 2026-10-27, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-80"></a>**DEC-UX-80** · A flowing gradient, drawn live with WebGL, can be the background in place of the background of either theme or of a picture, in a day and a night version for the two themes. It moves very slowly, in a loop of 60 seconds without a seam, and follows the mouse, eased over seconds; with "Reduce motion" it stands still. It is the one exception to CSS for the background, kept light: half resolution, 30 frames a second, paused while its tab is hidden. *Decided on 2026-10-27, by Max, after a test of `?bg=flow` made from reference pictures he chose.* *Note of 2026-10-28: the loop is 15 seconds now, at the request of Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-81"></a>**DEC-UX-81** · A Broken Lens, Report or query carries a red dot on its kind icon wherever it is listed: the panel, tiles, tables, search and the Queries of a Resource, for everyone who sees it, with what is missing on hover. On a tile and in its status row a red chip reads "Broken". Its page puts an urgent callout on top: what no longer exists and since which dbt run, that it works again by itself when that comes back, and for its Owner the way to repair it. Run is off, and the last result is not shown. Red, because nothing works until what is missing comes back or someone acts. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-DH-03](../overview/open-themes.md#op-dh-03)): "a red dot or something like this".*
- <a id="dec-ux-82"></a>**DEC-UX-82** · No screen shows a type of a Lens: not its page, not the Lenses of a Collection, not making a Lens, not the Lenses that Query suggests ([DEC-LE-06](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#dec-le-06)). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-34](#op-ux-34)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-83"></a>**DEC-UX-83** · The result on the page of a Lens is the grid of Query ([DEC-UX-72](#dec-ux-72)): one component, with the row number, a column per field with its type, columns made narrower or wider, 100 rows a page and a cell to copy. While its Parameters changed since the run, the grid fades. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-DH-11](../overview/open-themes.md#op-dh-11), [DEC-LE-08](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#dec-le-08)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-84"></a>**DEC-UX-84** · The initials at the foot of the rail open My page: who the User is in Data Hub, what they own, and their Excel keys, made, listed and revoked there. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-DH-25](../overview/open-themes.md#op-dh-25), [DEC-DH-79](../overview/data-hub.md#dec-dh-79)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-85"></a>**DEC-UX-85** · My page has two tabs: Profile, with who the User is, the language of Data Hub, what they own and their queries; and Excel keys. A User chooses their language in Profile. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-DH-46](../overview/open-themes.md#op-dh-46)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-86"></a>**DEC-UX-86** · The start screen of Collections shows nothing "popular" for now: no popular Collections and no popular Lenses, only new Lenses. What makes something popular is usage reporting, which is not part of v1 ([DEC-DH-75](../overview/data-hub.md#dec-dh-75)). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-01](#op-ux-01)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-87"></a>**DEC-UX-87** · The panel of Collections lists every Collection the User sees, filtered to All or Yours. "Shared resources" is no concept and goes: a Resource is always seen through its Collection ([DEC-DH-77](../overview/data-hub.md#dec-dh-77)). Only the Collections of the User say whether they are public or private. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-02](#op-ux-02)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-88"></a>**DEC-UX-88** · A run in the history of a query is one row: when it ran, with Preview and Apply. It does not open to what changed, what it read or its Query ID. Preview shows the run read only; Apply takes its SQL and values into the query. The last runs under a query in the panel show when they ran, too. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-28](#op-ux-28)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-89"></a>**DEC-UX-89** · The rail holds Collections, Query, Charts, Messages and the learning centre on top, and the theme and My page at the bottom. Reports have no icon and no start screen of their own: they live in their Collections, as Lenses do ([DEC-UX-66](#dec-ux-66)). Charts is the gallery of semgraph, the Chart Server ([DEC-PF-23](../requirements/technical/00-platform.md#dec-pf-23)). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-03](#op-ux-03)).* *Note of 2026-10-30: for the Data Team, Admin follows them ([DEC-UX-130](#dec-ux-130)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-90"></a>**DEC-UX-90** · The page of a Resource has the tabs Overview, Columns, Lineage, History and Preview. EDA is not in v1. Lineage is walked one step at a time, with the starting Resource pinned and the models in between in a dashed box, and never shown as a whole. History is every query that used the Resource, one row per query, a Lens or a plain query; it takes the place of the tab Queries. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-04](#op-ux-04)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-91"></a>**DEC-UX-91** · Data Hub is not made for small screens. Below 900 pixels wide it shows only one message: it is made for a computer screen, make the window wider or open it on a computer. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-06](#op-ux-06)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-92"></a>**DEC-UX-92** · The interface writes Defined Terms with capitals, as the documentation does: "New Collection", "Owner Unit", "Add Resources", "The Owner has been told". A word in its ordinary meaning, and a name a User gives, keep their own spelling. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-12](#op-ux-12)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-93"></a>**DEC-UX-93** · For now, the settings of a User stay in the browser: the panel open or shut, the dismissed intros, the theme, the background and the language. They do not follow the User to another device. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-13](#op-ux-13)).* *Note of 2026-10-28: replaced by [DEC-UX-96](#dec-ux-96): the settings are kept in Data Hub.*
- <a id="dec-ux-94"></a>**DEC-UX-94** · The backgrounds are a set per theme, and one is picked at random when Data Hub starts and again when the User switches between day and night; it stays for the session. Nobody chooses it, there is no default, and a User adds no picture of their own; the sign-in picks its photo the same way. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-20](#op-ux-20), [OP-UX-31](#op-ux-31)).* *Note of 2026-10-28: the User can choose one now; Random stays the default ([DEC-UX-108](#dec-ux-108)).* *Note of 2026-11-04: the sign-in picks a background of the set, not a photo ([DEC-UX-173](#dec-ux-173)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-95"></a>**DEC-UX-95** · In the Lineage, this Resource always stays in the middle, with an arrow to each direct downstream model and from each direct upstream model. Stepping to a model makes it the active one on its side, and the models between are a plain list in between. This refines the Lineage of [DEC-UX-90](#dec-ux-90). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max, on the first design.* *Note of 2026-10-28: refined by [DEC-UX-100](#dec-ux-100): columns are added, not folded into a list in between.*
- <a id="dec-ux-96"></a>**DEC-UX-96** · The settings of a User are kept in Data Hub, as a list of keys and values per User: the language, the theme, the panel open or shut, the dismissed intros and the like. They follow the User to another device, and Data Hub knows the language to send e-mail in. This replaces [DEC-UX-93](#dec-ux-93). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-35](#op-ux-35)).* *Note of 2026-11-04: the theme is no longer kept: it follows the system ([DEC-UX-172](#dec-ux-172)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-97"></a>**DEC-UX-97** · An error that keeps a query from running, a table outside its Resources or an `@name` that is not a Parameter, shows in the warning colour, as designed; the urgent red stays for what waits for the Data Team and a run above the approval threshold. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-26](#op-ux-26)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-98"></a>**DEC-UX-98** · The Preview of a Resource shows its rows in the grid of Query, as the page of a Lens does ([DEC-UX-83](#dec-ux-83)): one component for every result. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-99"></a>**DEC-UX-99** · The result of a run is kept as long as BigQuery keeps the result of its job, about a day. After that, the Preview of the run shows its SQL and values and offers "Run again", a new run of the query. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-27](#op-ux-27)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-100"></a>**DEC-UX-100** · In the Lineage, a click on a model adds its next models beside it as a column further out, and keeps what was there; nothing between is folded into a list. Every edge is a smooth curve with an arrowhead, from the right of a model to the left of the next, in the direction the data flows. This refines [DEC-UX-95](#dec-ux-95). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max, on the second design.* *Note of 2026-11-12: without an arrowhead now ([DEC-UX-215](#dec-ux-215)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-101"></a>**DEC-UX-101** · A deprecated Resource carries a chip "Deprecated" next to its Level wherever the Level shows, with the reason on hover; on its page a line under the status row says who deprecated it, when, why and what to use instead, and that it stays usable; in the panel a tag "Deprecated" stands next to its name. The Owner and the Data Team set it with "Deprecate" in the header, and take it away with "Take it away". A new Lens on it is possible, with a warning. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-25](#op-ux-25), [OP-SR-16](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#op-sr-16)).* *Note of 2026-11-04: deprecating moved from the header and its sheet to the Danger zone of the Overview ([DEC-UX-176](#dec-ux-176)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-102"></a>**DEC-UX-102** · In the Lineage, opening another branch closes the branch that was open: one flow at a time on each side. The columns have no titles, and every card has the same height, with a name on two lines at most. This refines [DEC-UX-100](#dec-ux-100). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-103"></a>**DEC-UX-103** · The glass and solid surfaces keep their values on the light background; they take no warmer tint. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-32](#op-ux-32)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-104"></a>**DEC-UX-104** · Data Hub has the look of this design: warm greys, colour only on content, and a light and a dark theme. It replaces the colours of the brandbook, with teal on larger areas, and the one light look of [DEC-PF-22](../requirements/technical/00-platform.md#dec-pf-22). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-09](#op-ux-09)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-105"></a>**DEC-UX-105** · A Collection has no colour: no stripe on its card, no line at the head of its page, no colour on its chip, and no colour to choose. Its grey icon is what sets it apart. This replaces [DEC-UX-15](#dec-ux-15) for the colour, [DEC-UX-60](#dec-ux-60) and [DEC-UX-68](#dec-ux-68). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([DEC-CO-23](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#dec-co-23)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-106"></a>**DEC-UX-106** · The head of a Lens shows the icon of its Level next to its name, without a chip or the name of the Level, which shows on hover; and no chip of its Collection, as a Lens is always seen in its Collection. Who made it and its tags move to the tab About. "Open in Query" stays for its Owner Unit only. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-107"></a>**DEC-UX-107** · A Data Admin changes the Grain and the mappings of a Resource with "Edit relations" on its Columns tab, next to "Edit", which opens step 2 of adding Resources for this one Resource. Nobody else sees it. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([OP-UX-36](#op-ux-36)).* *Note of 2026-11-16: it reopens the steps Model type, Grain and storage, and Fields and relations ([DEC-UX-262](#dec-ux-262), [DEC-CO-27](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#dec-co-27)).* *Note of 2026-11-25: the Grain has a button of its own wherever it shows, which keeps the model type ([DEC-UX-337](#dec-ux-337)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-108"></a>**DEC-UX-108** · The User chooses the background on My page, in a tab Background: two choices, one for day and one for night. The tab shows the backgrounds of the current theme only, Random first, the default, then each background with a small picture; to choose the other, the User switches the theme. The choices are settings of the User, kept in Data Hub ([DEC-UX-96](#dec-ux-96)). This replaces "nobody chooses it" of [DEC-UX-94](#dec-ux-94). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max; first with one choice that went back to Random on a switch of theme, then, the same day, one choice per theme.*
- <a id="dec-ux-109"></a>**DEC-UX-109** · The flowing backgrounds move at random, without a loop: every movement turns at a random speed of its own from a random start, so that they never repeat and differ each time Data Hub starts. Nine flowing lights join them, after pictures Max chose: four by night (Borealis, Embers, Dusk, Horizon) and five by day (Periwinkle, Sunrise, Apricot, Blush, Sky), each to choose on My page. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max, after a test by day. On 2026-11-09, at the request of Max, the first of the night was renamed Borealis, so that no background carries a first name.*
- <a id="dec-ux-110"></a>**DEC-UX-110** · Fusilli joins the backgrounds of the day: a photo of pasta that Max chose, shown as it is, not painted, and blurred by the frames as the other pictures are. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-04: Fusilli left the set ([DEC-UX-173](#dec-ux-173)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-111"></a>**DEC-UX-111** · On "Make it a lens", an author who owns no Collection makes one in the same screen: a tile in place of the choice of Collection, with its name, one of their groups and its icon, private by default. *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max ([DEC-CO-24](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#dec-co-24)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-112"></a>**DEC-UX-112** · Fresh piste joins the backgrounds of the day: a photo of a groomed ski slope at sunrise that Max chose, shown as it is, as Fusilli is ([DEC-UX-110](#dec-ux-110)). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-04: Fresh piste left the set ([DEC-UX-173](#dec-ux-173)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-113"></a>**DEC-UX-113** · Concrete joins the backgrounds of the day: a photo of a grey concrete wall that Max chose, shown as it is, as Fusilli is ([DEC-UX-110](#dec-ux-110)). *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-11: Concrete left the backgrounds ([DEC-UX-208](#dec-ux-208)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-114"></a>**DEC-UX-114** · A new query on Resources that no Lens uses says so in its right half, in one quiet line under the heading: "No Lens uses these Resources yet." Until Lenses are built, in part 6 of the [build order](build-order.md), every new query shows it; "Make it a lens" and "Lenses on these resources" are there, switched off. *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-115"></a>**DEC-UX-115** · A suggestion of a column in the editor of Query shows, beside the list, the type of its field and its descriptions, as everywhere a description is used ([FR-RC-18](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#fr-rc-18)). *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max, with [OP-SR-20](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#op-sr-20).*
- <a id="dec-ux-116"></a>**DEC-UX-116** · Above 1 GB, Run in Query becomes "Send to the Data Team" with what the query reads, and opens a pop-out over the Pre-flight bar: "Very large: one run reads more than 1 GB; someone of the Data Team accepts it first", an optional word for the Data Team, and Send. Once sent, the bar says since when it waits for the Data Team, and the query in the panel gets the label "To the Data Team", as drawn. The Request is [MSG-QRY-01](../requirements/functional/message-catalogue.md#msg-qry-01); the Data Team answers it in the Message Centre, part 7 of the [build order](build-order.md). *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max ([DEC-SR-36](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#dec-sr-36)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-117"></a>**DEC-UX-117** · In v1 the list of a Choice, or of Several choices, is typed, one value a line. A list that comes from the values of a column comes later. *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-118"></a>**DEC-UX-118** · Data Hub says what a function gives today only for the functions the field suggests, which it works out itself: today, today minus 3 days, the start of this week, month, quarter or year, and the end of the last complete week or month. Next to any other function it says "worked out at every run". Every function goes into the SQL of the run as it is written, in place of its Parameter, so the Pre-flight checks it as it checks the rest of the SQL. *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max ([DEC-LE-14](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#dec-le-14)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-119"></a>**DEC-UX-119** · A click on the name or the question of a query, above the editor, edits it in place, with Save and Cancel, as the head of a Collection is edited. Only who made the query changes them; a name stays required. *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max ([OP-UX-42](#op-ux-42)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-120"></a>**DEC-UX-120** · The Preview of a Resource has no tile for a regular export: under the table there is only the way to Query. A structural export still goes to the Data Team ([FR-CH-02](../requirements/functional/13-channels.md#fr-ch-02)). *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-121"></a>**DEC-UX-121** · Making a Lens asks what it does, step by step: a field under "What it shows", a step a line, in plain language, which its author writes and changes when editing it. The tab About of the Lens shows the steps as a numbered list, written by its author. This replaces "no step-by-step description" in [DEC-UX-75](#dec-ux-75) ([DEC-LE-16](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#dec-le-16)). *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-122"></a>**DEC-UX-122** · The result on the page of a Lens has no "Download CSV" in v1 ([DEC-LE-17](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#dec-le-17)). *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-123"></a>**DEC-UX-123** · "New Lens" on the page of a Collection, for its Owner Unit and the Data Team, opens a new query; "Make it a lens" on that query offers this Collection first ([FR-CO-29](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#fr-co-29)). *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max ([OP-UX-43](#op-ux-43)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-124"></a>**DEC-UX-124** · A Parameter of the kind Choice has no value "all" in v1: its default is one of its values, as in Query, and a query that may read every value uses Several choices ([DEC-UX-71](#dec-ux-71)). *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max ([OP-UX-44](#op-ux-44)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-125"></a>**DEC-UX-125** · A Lens that stopped because a run with its defaults grew past 1 GB ([DEC-LE-11](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#dec-le-11)) stays where it is listed, for everyone who sees it. Its page shows a chip "Stopped" next to its Level and a callout in the warning colour on top: what one run reads now, since when, that it waits for the Data Team and runs for nobody until then, and for its Owner Unit "Open its query". Run is off, and its last result is not shown. As Broken, in amber: nothing is missing, it only waits. *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-126"></a>**DEC-UX-126** · "New Lenses" on the start screen of Collections shows the Lenses published in the last 30 days in a public Collection, whose Resources the User may all use, newest first, at most six tiles. Those of the User's own Owner Units are under "Yours", not here. With none, the section says so in one line. *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-127"></a>**DEC-UX-127** · The search on the start screen of Collections takes a question, sent with Enter, and shows in place of the sections what shares its words: Lenses first, as each answers a question, then Resources, then Collections; in each group those that share most words first, the shared words marked; only what the User may see. "Clear" goes back to the start screen. When nothing shares a word, it says so, with "New query". As drawn in [collections-start](screens/collections-start.html) (`?search=results`, `?search=none`). *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max ([OP-UX-45](#op-ux-45)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-128"></a>**DEC-UX-128** · A refused Request on a Lens shows the decision of the Data Team as its reply, with the reason. The Lens stays unpublished, seen only by its author and the Data Team, and does not run; it is no longer frozen. Its page says "Refused by the Data Team", by whom and when, with "Open its query", where "Update the lens" checks its size again: within range it is published, above 1 GB it waits again with a new Request ([MSG-CNV-05](../requirements/functional/message-catalogue.md#msg-cnv-05), [FR-LE-09](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#fr-le-09)). *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max: drawn in [lens-new-context](screens/lens-new-context.html) (`?state=refused`).*
- <a id="dec-ux-129"></a>**DEC-UX-129** · A notification outside Data Hub shows the Message as the Message Catalogue writes it, in a frame of Data Hub. The e-mail comes from Data Hub, with the first line of the plain text as its subject; it shows the mark and the name, the object with its kind and Collection, the laid-out text, "Open in context" as its one button, and at the foot why it came, the way to the Message Centre and that replies are not read; always light, in the language of its receiver, Dutch for a group. The Slack direct message shows the same text, "Open in context" and the object under it. As drawn in [notifications](screens/notifications.html) ([FR-CV-15](../requirements/functional/14-conversations.md#fr-cv-15)). *Decided on 2026-10-29, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-130"></a>**DEC-UX-130** · The rail has a section for the Data Team, Admin, which only a Data Admin sees. Its panel lists its tools, which will grow; the first is Webhooks: the Source Jobs of the environment and every delivery of its webhook, with what Data Hub did with it ([FR-AR-18](../requirements/functional/01-artifact-registry.md#fr-ar-18), [FR-AR-19](../requirements/functional/01-artifact-registry.md#fr-ar-19)). Screen: [admin-webhooks](screens/admin-webhooks.html). *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-131"></a>**DEC-UX-131** · "Add to a collection" on a Resource that waits opens the sheet that adds Resources to a Collection ([FR-CO-31](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#fr-co-31)), with a first step of its own: the choice of the Collection, searchable, each with its Owner Unit, how many Resources it holds and whether it is public; the Resource is ticked. The second step, its Grain and relations, is the same as from the page of a Collection. Built as soon as it is drawn, so that the Data Team can add the Resources that came in on uat. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-132"></a>**DEC-UX-132** · The sheet that adds Resources to a Collection adds one Resource at a time: its first step chooses one Resource with a radio button, not several with check boxes, and its second step shows the Grain and relations of that one Resource. To add another, the Data Admin opens the sheet again. It narrows "one or more" of [FR-CO-31](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#fr-co-31). *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-133"></a>**DEC-UX-133** · "Without a collection" in the panel of Collections is folded by default: its number in the red badge says that Resources wait, and the Data Admin unfolds it to see them. Opening one of its Resources unfolds it, as a Collection unfolds when one of its Resources is open, and so does a search that finds one. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-134"></a>**DEC-UX-134** · Next to the heading Lenses on the page of a Collection, the action is called "Add a Lens: start a query" ("Lens toevoegen: start een query"), not "New Lens": it says that a Lens is made from a query, which it opens ([DEC-UX-123](#dec-ux-123)). The primary action in the head keeps "New Lens". *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-20: a Lens is started from New › Lens; no action stands next to a heading any more ([DEC-UX-310](#dec-ux-310)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-135"></a>**DEC-UX-135** · On My page, the list of the User's groups keeps to about six rows and scrolls within itself, so that a User in many groups does not stretch the page. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-136"></a>**DEC-UX-136** · The Message Centre opens on Unread instead of For you: what moved since the User last looked comes first. When nothing is unread, it says "Nothing to catch up on"; For you stays one click away in the panel. A Message the User writes is read for them at once, so it never waits there. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-137"></a>**DEC-UX-137** · The panel of Query leaves out previews, the queries of Lenses and the queries the User hid, until they switch on the chip for them under the search: Previews, Lenses, Hidden, each with how many there are. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.* *Note of 2026-10-30: the chips became a filter that folds open under the search ([DEC-UX-150](#dec-ux-150)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-138"></a>**DEC-UX-138** · A User hides a query from their panel with the eye that shows when the pointer is on it, or with "Hide" on its page, and shows it again the same way, from Hidden. It is hidden for that User alone: the Data Team's All changes for nobody else. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-14: "Hide" is no longer on the page of a query; the eye in the panel and the quick window hide it ([DEC-UX-246](#dec-ux-246)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-139"></a>**DEC-UX-139** · The panel of Query groups its queries per day in the current week, per week back to the first day of last month, then per month, each period with how many queries it holds. Only today is open and read; another period is read when the User opens it. A search looks through all of them. It replaces the grouping per day of the panel. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.* *Changed on 2026-11-20, by Max: today starts folded too, as the other periods ("the today may be closed by default").*
- <a id="dec-ux-140"></a>**DEC-UX-140** · In the panel of Query, the last runs of the query that is open stay folded until the User unfolds them with its arrow. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-141"></a>**DEC-UX-141** · The SQL editor offers templates among its suggestions, marked "Template": "select … from", "where true and" and "where false or", each written on its own lines with the cursor where the User types next. Typing "select" or "where" brings them up. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-14: "select … from" ends on `where true` ([DEC-UX-244](#dec-ux-244)).* *Note of 2026-10-30: also "with … as", which opens a CTE with its name first, and "final as … order by", which closes a query on a last CTE `final`; typing "with" or "final" brings them up (Max).*
- <a id="dec-ux-142"></a>**DEC-UX-142** · Cmd+Enter, or Ctrl+Enter on Windows, in the SQL editor does what the button that runs the query does: it runs a query that reads up to 100 MB; above that it puts the focus on the button, which asks for the approval; what the Pre-flight blocks, or what goes to the Data Team, does not run. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-18: both run without waiting for the Pre-flight of what was typed ([DEC-UX-280](#dec-ux-280)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-143"></a>**DEC-UX-143** · The page of a query has two layouts, chosen with two icons in its head and kept in the settings of the User: the query above its result, the default, or the query next to it, with its Parameters and its Pre-flight bar under it on the left and its result on the right. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-144"></a>**DEC-UX-144** · The page of a query fills the height of the screen: the query and its result each scroll on their own, the Pre-flight bar stays at the foot of the query, and the divider between them is dragged, or moved with the arrow keys, between a fifth and four fifths, to give one more room. Where it stands is kept in the browser, per layout. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-145"></a>**DEC-UX-145** · The head of the page of a Collection shows no "New Lens": "Add a Lens: start a query" next to the heading Lenses does that ([DEC-UX-134](#dec-ux-134)). Its row of chips shows the Owner Unit and whether it is public, not how many Resources it holds nor when and by whom it was made. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-20: the head has one "New", whose menu holds Lens ([DEC-UX-310](#dec-ux-310)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-146"></a>**DEC-UX-146** · The window out of focus, the panel or the main area, fades whole, its text with it, rather than only its reading surfaces becoming softer. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-147"></a>**DEC-UX-147** · The SQL editor takes all the height the query has left, and scrolls in itself. With the query above its result, its Parameters stand to the right of the editor; with the query next to its result, under it. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.* *Note of 2026-10-30: the editor is the only part of the query that scrolls, and always has the most room: nothing stands under the Pre-flight bar, and next to the result the Parameters start folded to their line of chips (Max).*
- <a id="dec-ux-148"></a>**DEC-UX-148** · After `from` and `join`, the SQL editor suggests the Resources of the query by their Display Name and writes the full name of their table, quoted; last comes "New Resource…", which opens the sheet that starts a new query with another Resource, as a query reads only its own ([DEC-UX-73](#dec-ux-73)). *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-19: "New Resource…" adds it to this query ([DEC-UX-287](#dec-ux-287)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-149"></a>**DEC-UX-149** · Switching between day and night happens at once: every transition is off until the new colours are on screen, so no surface, text or background slides from one into the other. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-150"></a>**DEC-UX-150** · What the panel of Query also shows, Previews, Lenses and Hidden, is ticked in a filter rather than chips, one component for every panel that filters what it shows: a button next to the search, with the number of kinds it adds, folds open a small form with a checkbox for each, what it is and how many there are, "Reset", and "Only your panel changes". Folded, one line under the search says what it also shows, each with a cross that takes it away. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max, from a proposal.*
- <a id="dec-ux-151"></a>**DEC-UX-151** · The panel of Collections has the same filter next to its search ([DEC-UX-150](#dec-ux-150)): Resources, Lenses or both, both by default, each with how many there are in All or Yours; one always stays ticked. With one left out, a Collection without the other leaves the panel, "Without a collection" with the Resources; with only Lenses, each Collection unfolds to them. Folded, the line under the search says what is not shown. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-152"></a>**DEC-UX-152** · The Lineage is drawn on the canvas of Svelte Flow, in the colours of Data Hub, and keeps its curves and its walk one step at a time ([DEC-UX-100](#dec-ux-100), [DEC-UX-102](#dec-ux-102)). The canvas pans when dragged and zooms with its buttons at the bottom left, a pinch, or Cmd or Ctrl and scrolling; scrolling alone still scrolls the page. Each step brings what was walked into view, shrunk to half at most; when even that is too wide, the Resource stays in view with the columns nearest it, on the side that was walked. *Decided on 2026-10-30, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-153"></a>**DEC-UX-153** · Sunlit peaks joins the backgrounds of the day: a photo from Max of snowy peaks under a blue sky, shown as it is and covering the window, as Fresh piste is ([DEC-UX-112](#dec-ux-112)). It stands still: wind drawn over it, mist drifting and snow blowing at random on a canvas, was tried and not kept. *Decided on 2026-10-31, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-04: Sunlit peaks left the set ([DEC-UX-173](#dec-ux-173)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-154"></a>**DEC-UX-154** · The bar at the foot of a query keeps one line of a fixed height, which shows the Pre-flight when the query can run and what stops it, such as an error of BigQuery, when it cannot; the two cross-fade, the one going out a little quicker than the one coming in, so that the query keeps its height while it is typed. The Pre-flight waits until typing pauses two seconds; what is not typing is checked at once, and Cmd+Enter does not wait. *Decided on 2026-10-31, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-18: neither does the button Run ([DEC-UX-280](#dec-ux-280)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-155"></a>**DEC-UX-155** · After `from` and `join`, the SQL editor also suggests every other Resource the User may use, after those of the query, each once and marked "new query". Picking one writes its name, as for those of the query, and opens the sheet that starts a new query with it added, taking the SQL and Parameters along, as a query reads only its own Resources ([DEC-UX-73](#dec-ux-73)). A query of someone else, or one with ten Resources, suggests only its own. This extends [DEC-UX-148](#dec-ux-148). *Decided on 2026-10-31, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-19: marked "adds it", it is added to this query ([DEC-UX-287](#dec-ux-287)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-156"></a>**DEC-UX-156** · A Collection keeps its Resources and Lenses in folders: each folder, Resource and Lens sits at the top of the Collection or in one folder of it, and a folder holds folders, as deep as needed. The panel shows them as a tree, each folder unfolding to what it holds; the page of a Collection shows only what sits at its top, with its folders, and a folder has a page of its own with what it holds. New Resources and Lenses come in at the top. The Owner Unit and the Data Team make, rename, move and delete folders; Delete stays on, and on a folder that is not empty it warns and deletes nothing. *Decided on 2026-10-31, by Max, on the design of [collection-folder](screens/collection-folder.html).*
- <a id="dec-ux-157"></a>**DEC-UX-157** · Something moves to another folder by dragging it, a whole row, onto that folder: in the list of a folder or a Collection, in the panel, or in the path above the page. Rows are no links: a click anywhere on a row opens what it is, and a drag anywhere on it moves it. No tick boxes and no "Move to…". Once dropped, a short line on screen says where it went. *Decided on 2026-10-31, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-158"></a>**DEC-UX-158** · A Collection has an intro, written by its Owner Unit or the Data Team in Markdown: an editor with buttons for a heading, bold, italic, a list and a link, and a preview. It is shown under the head of the page of the Collection, as "About this Collection". *Decided on 2026-10-31, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-159"></a>**DEC-UX-159** · The page of a Lens has "Open in Excel" in its head, for everyone who sees the Lens. It opens a sheet with the address of the Lens for Power Query, to copy, and the three steps in Excel: From web with the address, Basic with the e-mail and an Excel key, and load; with what Excel gets, at most 10,000 rows with the Parameters at their defaults, and a link to My page for a key ([DEC-CH-06](../requirements/functional/13-channels.md#dec-ch-06)). *Decided on 2026-10-31, by Max, on the design of [lens](screens/lens.html).*
- <a id="dec-ux-160"></a>**DEC-UX-160** · A Broken query shows as a Broken Lens does: Run is off, a callout on top names what no longer exists and since which dbt run, and says that changing the SQL, or the return of what it reads, makes it work again; it has the red dot in the panel of Query ([FR-SR-53](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#fr-sr-53), [DEC-UX-81](#dec-ux-81)). *Decided on 2026-10-31, by Max, on the design of [query](screens/query.html).*
- <a id="dec-ux-161"></a>**DEC-UX-161** · What a panel lists, All or Yours, is no switch of its own above the list: it is the first choice in the filter next to the search ([DEC-UX-150](#dec-ux-150)). In the panel of Collections, "Which Collections": All, by default, or Yours ([DEC-UX-87](#dec-ux-87)); in the panel of Query, for the Data Team only, "Whose queries": Yours, by default, or All ([DEC-UX-20](#dec-ux-20)). The button counts it as one difference when it is not the default, the folded line under the search names it with a cross that puts it back, and "Reset" puts it back too. *Decided on 2026-10-31, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-162"></a>**DEC-UX-162** · What sits in a Collection is edited in place as a query is ([DEC-UX-119](#dec-ux-119)): a click on the name or the description of a Collection or a folder, or on the name of a Lens, turns them into fields, with Save and Cancel; Escape cancels too, only what changed is saved, and a name stays required. Only who may change them can click: the Owner Unit and the Data Team, and nobody while a Lens waits for the Data Team. Without a description, they see "Add a description" in its place. The rest stays where it was: "Edit all" on a Collection, for its Owner Unit, who sees it, its icon and intro; "Edit more" on a Lens, for what it shows, its steps, tags and the names of its Parameters. A folder has a description of its own, plain text of at most 500 characters, as a Collection has ([FR-CO-42](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#fr-co-42)). The name of a Resource comes from the label of its exposure in dbt ([DEC-SR-24](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#dec-sr-24)), so its page stays as it is. *Decided on 2026-11-02, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-20: on the page of a Collection, "Edit all" is "Edit more", next to Save and Cancel; the head has no Edit button ([DEC-UX-309](#dec-ux-309)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-163"></a>**DEC-UX-163** · The panel of Query has "Refresh" in its head, next to "New query": it reads the queries again, such as those Claude made through the MCP while the page was open, which the panel otherwise shows only once it is opened again. *Decided on 2026-11-02, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-164"></a>**DEC-UX-164** · An MCP App of one Collection shows the icon of that Collection before its name in its head, as the panel does: a Resource, its Preview and its publication, and the result of a query whose Resources all sit in one Collection. A query over several Collections shows none. The MCP App loads nothing from outside, so mcp sends the drawing of the icon with the data of the window. *Decided on 2026-11-02, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-165"></a>**DEC-UX-165** · Whatever is read shows three dots going back and forth in its place until it is there: a page, a panel, a tab, a sheet, a tile or a count. They show after 300 ms, so that a quick answer shows none, and stand still with Reduce motion. Screen readers hear "Loading". *Decided on 2026-11-02, by Max: "three dots going back and forth is fine".*
- <a id="dec-ux-166"></a>**DEC-UX-166** · On Webhooks a Data Admin follows a dbt project and stops following one, as well as changing its Source Job: "Follow a dbt project" in the head of Source Jobs opens a sheet with the dbt projects of the account not followed yet and, once one is chosen, its jobs; "Stop following" on a tile asks to confirm in its place, and says that its Resources stay as they are. A job that does not generate the docs cannot be chosen, as a CI job cannot, and says why. *Decided on 2026-11-02, by Max, with [DEC-AR-10](../requirements/functional/01-artifact-registry.md#dec-ar-10).*
- <a id="dec-ux-167"></a>**DEC-UX-167** · The list of deliveries on Webhooks keeps to the height of the screen and scrolls within itself, its filter on top, as the groups on My page do ([DEC-UX-135](#dec-ux-135)); the delivery opened stays beside it. *Decided on 2026-11-02, by Max: the list ran under the screen.*
- <a id="dec-ux-168"></a>**DEC-UX-168** · The Overview of a Resource is split into parts, each a page of its own, with a navigation on the left under the tabs: Summary, the page it opens on, then Description, Known Behaviour, Known Gaps, Querying, Entities, Maturity and Changes. A part shows a count or a dot when something in it calls for a look. Each page the Owner and the Data Team may edit has its own Edit. Screen: [resource-overview](screens/resource-overview.html). *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: one page of tiles held too much.* *Note of 2026-11-04: the Danger zone closes the parts, for the Owner Unit and the Data Team ([DEC-UX-176](#dec-ux-176)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-169"></a>**DEC-UX-169** · The Summary of a Resource numbers its parts in the order to read them, each with its weight, as a label, and a way to the part itself; the four labels are explained above the parts. A deprecation comes first, in the signal colour; a part with nothing in it is left out. It shows no separate text of what Claude reads: Claude reads the same Summary ([FR-CL-25](../requirements/functional/17-claude.md#fr-cl-25)). *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-170"></a>**DEC-UX-170** · The Known Gaps of a Resource are a part of their own on its Overview: a list, one gap per line, each with who added it and when. The Owner and the Data Team add a gap in a line at the bottom, and change or remove one on its own line, without editing the whole list. *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-04: a gap may be narrowed down by a period, fields and rows ([DEC-UX-177](#dec-ux-177)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-171"></a>**DEC-UX-171** · The communication on a Resource is part of its Summary, not a part of its own in the navigation: "Messages" in the header suffices, and replies are written in its slide-in, as before ([DEC-UX-67](#dec-ux-67)). The Summary takes the open Signals and the Q&A of the Owner and the Data Team, and what colleagues wrote that they have not touched yet, with the role of the Owner and the Data Team next to their names. *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: the communication on a Resource belongs in its Overview; the Messages in the header suffice to read them all.*
- <a id="dec-ux-172"></a>**DEC-UX-172** · The theme always follows the system, also while Data Hub is open. The switch at the foot of the rail turns it for the session only and is not remembered: the next change of the system, or opening Data Hub again, follows the system. The switch shows where it goes: the sun by night, the moon by day. This replaces "the choice is remembered" of the themes. *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-173"></a>**DEC-UX-173** · Ski lift, Alpenglow, Spring snow, Fusilli, Fresh piste and Sunlit peaks leave the backgrounds of the day, and Lights on those of the night. The sign-in shows a background of the set of its theme, picked at random as in the app, in place of a photo with its story. *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-174"></a>**DEC-UX-174** · A sheet takes over the whole window: its backdrop covers the rail, the panel and the page, stays where it is, and dims them more deeply than before, so that nothing behind it can be clicked or scrolled. The sheet takes half the width of the window, more where its content needs it. *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max, after a sheet over Webhooks left the page below it open.*
- <a id="dec-ux-175"></a>**DEC-UX-175** · The Maturity part of the Overview explains every Level beside the ladder: the Levels passed, marked so, with how and when they were reached; the one the Resource is at; and for those still to reach what it takes. The Publication has no tile of its own: who published and confirmed it, and when, stands with 3 · Confirmed. *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-176"></a>**DEC-UX-176** · Deprecating a Resource moves from the header to a part of its own at the end of the Overview, the Danger zone, for its Owner Unit and the Data Team: what deprecating does and why, explained, and its form on the page, in place of a sheet. *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: "a new button, 'danger zone' or something like that".*
- <a id="dec-ux-177"></a>**DEC-UX-177** · A Known Gap shows its scope as chips under its text, one per kind: Period, Fields and Rows. Adding or changing one is a small form on the list: its text, then each kind added as a block of its own, removed with its ×. The Summary writes the scope on one line under its gap; Columns marks a field a gap names under Fields. *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: a period on any date field, the partition by default; values typed freely; and Columns marks the field.* *Note of 2026-11-04: a period has no field of its own any more ([DEC-UX-178](#dec-ux-178)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-178"></a>**DEC-UX-178** · The period of a Known Gap is a from and a to, with no field of its own. Which fields a gap concerns is said by Fields, combined with the period as needed; the period is the time the data covers. This replaces "a period on any date field, the partition by default" of [DEC-UX-177](#dec-ux-177). Screen: [resource-overview](screens/resource-overview.html). *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: "a field on a period is odd when you can select fields; the combination does it".*
- <a id="dec-ux-179"></a>**DEC-UX-179** · An open Question or Signal has "Close" at the top right of the Message, for its writer, the Owner Unit of its object and the Data Team: an optional line why, then closed, with who, when and why in the conversation, and "Reopen" for the same people. A reply leaves it closed. The list has the state Closed and a chip for it. Screen: [messages](screens/messages.html) (`?close=closing`, `?close=closed`). *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: "the possibility for anyone in a message to close the message (sender, owner or data team)", Questions and Signals, with a Reopen button, a notification to the writer and an optional note.*
- <a id="dec-ux-180"></a>**DEC-UX-180** · An MCP App fits Claude on a phone: every frame lays out its own width, one column, nothing wider than the frame, a table of data scrolling in its own box, tabs and buttons at least 44 px high, and nothing told only on hover. Screens: the MCP Apps, with `?width=phone` ([mcp-resource](screens/mcp-resource.html)). *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: "seen that the mcp-app will also be included in the claude-mobile app, the responsiveness of these cards is really important".*
- <a id="dec-ux-181"></a>**DEC-UX-181** · Every MCP App folds to its head with a small button at its top right, and unfolds with it again. Screens: the MCP Apps, with `?fold=on` ([mcp-resource](screens/mcp-resource.html)). *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: "a collapse button so you can minimize the result, just a little top-right button".*
- <a id="dec-ux-182"></a>**DEC-UX-182** · Charts, the gallery of semgraph, is built as [charts](screens/charts.html) draws it. The panel lists the charts semgraph draws by the question they answer, Over time, Comparing, Now and the target, and Spread, each with its icon and name, and Charts opens on the first. One opened shows its name and the question it answers; its examples to switch between, each as semgraph draws it in the theme of the page, with its warning or caution, or why semgraph refuses it; and beside it the questions it is for, what to use instead, what it needs and where a User meets it. *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: "the graph pages, is it already built? if not this may also be included in this go".* *Note of 2026-11-04: an example keeps its own size, centred ([DEC-UX-183](#dec-ux-183)), and its hover works ([DEC-UX-184](#dec-ux-184)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-183"></a>**DEC-UX-183** · An example in Charts is shown at the size semgraph draws it, centred on its card: never stretched to the width of the card, only made smaller where the card is narrower than the picture. Screen: [charts](screens/charts.html). *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: "it now takes 100%, which stretches the chart; just centring it at its true size is perfect".*
- <a id="dec-ux-184"></a>**DEC-UX-184** · An example in Charts is the SVG semgraph drew, placed in the page itself, not shown as a picture: its hover, its metadata panels and the links in its footer work as semgraph made them, with the mouse, by touch and with Tab. Data Hub leaves out of it anything that could run, and its styles change nothing else on the page. This replaces "as a picture" of [DEC-UX-182](#dec-ux-182). Screen: [charts](screens/charts.html). *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: "the svg charts aren't interactive in the hub (hover isn't working)".*
- <a id="dec-ux-185"></a>**DEC-UX-185** · A Lens is deleted from its Edit, for its Owner Unit and the Data Team: a small button under Save, "Delete this lens…", opens a sheet with what deleting does, explained first, with how many colleagues used it in the last 30 days, also from Excel; why, optional; and "Keep it" or "Delete for good". The address of a deleted Lens says so to whoever saw it. Screens: [lens-edit](screens/lens-edit.html), [lens-deleted](screens/lens-deleted.html). *Decided on 2026-11-04, by Max: "a 'delete lens' button under edit lens > delete"; first drawn as a Danger zone at the foot of Edit, then, the same day, "a small button underneath 'bewaren' … a modal/slide-in with this content".*
- <a id="dec-ux-186"></a>**DEC-UX-186** · A User is in one Permission group. Something only some Users of a group need is a group of its own, made with "Duplicate". *Decided on 2026-11-06, by Max: "ok for all seven proposals" ([OP-UX-55](#op-ux-55)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-187"></a>**DEC-UX-187** · Permission groups replace the Role Groups: the Permission group of a User says what they may do, given to them in Data Hub, on Users; their Owner Units, still Workspace groups, say on which Collections. The Role Groups `datahub-<role>@` stop giving roles: when Permission groups start, the members of `datahub-datateam@` get the Data Team, and everyone else the default. What this changes in [FR-UM-11](../requirements/functional/02-user-management.md#fr-um-11) and [DEC-DH-78](../overview/data-hub.md#dec-dh-78) is worked out with the Permissions ([OP-PL-02](../requirements/functional/12-permissions-and-library-cards.md#op-pl-02)). *Decided on 2026-11-06, by Max: "ok for all seven proposals" ([OP-UX-56](#op-ux-56)).* *Note of 2026-11-06: at the start, every User who is not of the Data Team gets Business analyst, so that nothing changes for them, and the default for new Users is Lens user ([DEC-PL-07](../requirements/functional/12-permissions-and-library-cards.md#dec-pl-07)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-188"></a>**DEC-UX-188** · Nobody locks Data Hub out: the Permission group Data Team can do everything, cannot be changed or deleted, and keeps at least one User; nobody changes their own Permission group. *Decided on 2026-11-06, by Max: "ok for all seven proposals" ([OP-UX-57](#op-ux-57)).* *Note of 2026-11-06: the group is called Admin, as the Data Admins of today ([OP-PL-02](../requirements/functional/12-permissions-and-library-cards.md#op-pl-02)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-189"></a>**DEC-UX-189** · A change of Permission group, or of the Permissions of a group, takes effect within 15 minutes, when the Access Token of the User is renewed, in the web app, in Claude and in Excel; the sheet of a User and the edit bar of a group say so. *Decided on 2026-11-06, by Max: "ok for all seven proposals" ([OP-UX-58](#op-ux-58)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-190"></a>**DEC-UX-190** · Users lists only who signed in. A new User gets the default Permission group at their first sign-in, and the Data Team changes it after; adding a User by address beforehand comes later, if it is needed. *Decided on 2026-11-06, by Max: "ok for all seven proposals" ([OP-UX-59](#op-ux-59)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-191"></a>**DEC-UX-191** · A User sees their own Permission group, read-only, on My page: its name and what it allows, so that they know why a button is not there for them. It is still to be drawn. *Decided on 2026-11-06, by Max: "ok for all seven proposals" ([OP-UX-60](#op-ux-60)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-192"></a>**DEC-UX-192** · A User gets a Message when the Data Team moves them to another Permission group, and when their group is deleted, with what changed for them; not when the Permissions of their whole group change, which the Data Team announces itself. *Decided on 2026-11-06, by Max: "ok for all seven proposals" ([OP-UX-61](#op-ux-61)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-193"></a>**DEC-UX-193** · For a User whose Permission group shows results in a card only, the MCP App of a result says so in one line under the foot of the grid, in the muted colour, with the crossed eye: "Claude does not read these rows". The Preview of a Resource in Claude has the same line under its grid. Claude answers from the shape of the result, its columns, its number of rows, its Levels, and offers to help with a Signal ([FR-CL-28](../requirements/functional/17-claude.md#fr-cl-28)). Drawn in [mcp-query](screens/mcp-query.html), "In a card only". *Decided on 2026-11-06, by Max ([OP-UX-62](#op-ux-62)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-195"></a>**DEC-UX-195** · Building a Monitor shows the picture of the monitor above its steps, and the page of a Monitor shows it whole: a strip of tiles that is conceptual, not the data, and gains one layer per step, from how often to the reactions, with a row per measure and its boundaries under it, and the sentence that reads it back. Values in it are not counted: three shades stand for many values, and a line or a bar for a period. Drawn in [monitor-build](screens/monitor-build.html) and [monitor](screens/monitor.html). *Decided on 2026-11-07, by Max, in the brainstorm on the screens: "it is conceptual, not precise". Refined the same day, by Max: more graphical and abstract, equal tiles rather than bars, days in weeks, M T W T F S S, with the weekend greyer. Refined in the review of the picture, recorded on 2026-11-09, by Max: a background in weeks of its own; what it is compared with hollow, "the border with white fill", everywhere; everything before open on the left only; the strip always ends with today, and what is still coming in is dotted and empty; a filter shows like the values, the left-out ones dotted, "not transparent"; the boundaries of each measure as a gauge under the strip; reactions per boundary; and the weeks grouped by month, "inclusive the months/weeks view".* *Note of 2026-11-10: a period is a dot, or a dot with a tail, no longer a line or a bar ([DEC-UX-206](#dec-ux-206)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-196"></a>**DEC-UX-196** · A monitor is changed by clicking the parts of its picture, not by dragging them: a hover says what a part is, a click opens the floating window of a Parameter with its choices, the picture shows the change at once, Save keeps it and × or Esc undoes it. Drawn in [monitor](screens/monitor.html) (?mtab=breakdowns). *Decided by Max, in the review of the picture, recorded on 2026-11-09: "would it be possible to edit based upon this screen"; "the dragging isn't required, I would open modals so always click and then set up options, it is too difficult conceptually to drag, I would like hovers"; "the popups should be the same as we always use, the one of a Parameter in a query".*
- <a id="dec-ux-197"></a>**DEC-UX-197** · The values in the picture of a monitor are three shades of olive, the tokens `--seg-1` to `--seg-3`, light to strong, the strong one lighter in the dark theme. They stand for several values, never one each, and are not a Tone. *Decided by Max, recorded on 2026-11-09, from four options drawn side by side with the Tones (plum, olive, violet, and one dark colour in three shapes): "b", olive. Greys were drawn first and turned down: "grey colors are not ok". Olive is the family nearest the ochre of attention; on the picture they do not meet in one mark, as the values are lines and dots on the strip and a Tone is a hatch and an arrow on the gauge.*
- <a id="dec-ux-198"></a>**DEC-UX-198** · Prism joins the backgrounds of the day: a new kind, painted shapes, many kinds of shapes and many colours over each other, slowly moving, drawn with WebGL. It answers that the light backgrounds were too limited. *Decided on 2026-11-09, by Max: "I find the light backgrounds we have today quite limited, I would like some variations on them", after a picture he shared in which "many different kinds of shapes are used together, also many more colours"; on the draft: "This is perfect. You may build this." Renamed on 2026-11-09 to Prism, at the request of Max, so that no background carries the name of a colleague.*
- <a id="dec-ux-199"></a>**DEC-UX-199** · Nine more painted scenes join the backgrounds, after pictures Max shared: by day Cinnamon, Evening, Citrus, Orchid and Tide, by night Afterglow, Lumen, Cobalt and Midnight. *Decided on 2026-11-09, by Max, after drafts of each: "build them all". What he liked in them: of the brown one, "the two opposite sides, the dark also being on top"; of Afterglow, "the red line separating the two", with "the green peeping in between"; of Lumen, "a proper mix of all these different colours", light yellow in the middle; of Cobalt, "all these different tints of light blue that mix up"; of Midnight, "very subtle colours, but still present", matched together strongly. Renamed the same day, at the request of Max, so that no background carries a first name: the one with light yellow in the middle is Lumen.*
- <a id="dec-ux-200"></a>**DEC-UX-200** · Groups are kept in Data Hub, on a page of their own, Groups, reached from My page, no longer in Google Workspace: they no longer depend on the directory, which moves from Google to Entra after Data Hub goes live. The Owner Unit of a Collection is one of these groups ([DEC-UM-10](../requirements/functional/02-user-management.md#dec-um-10)). *Decided on 2026-11-10, by Max: "we are moving from google to entra, seen this shift and the fact that nobody is in production I think it is best to move."*
- <a id="dec-ux-201"></a>**DEC-UX-201** · Who changes a group: a User whose Permission group allows Make groups (`groups.manage`) makes one, and chooses which of their groups manages it; the members of that managing group change it. Whoever makes a Collection chooses which of their groups is its Owner Unit. A Data Admin, with Manage every group (`groups.all.manage`), changes every group. *Decided on 2026-11-10, by Max: "groups can be created by anyone with group.manage permission. when a group gets created the user chooses which of his groups applies as admin group for the group. when a collection gets created the user chooses which of his groups applies as admin group for the collection. data-admins have the permission groups.all.manage and can edit all groups."*
- <a id="dec-ux-204"></a>**DEC-UX-204** · The first step of a new monitor is a search over the Resources the User may manage, by name, Collection or field, with a row per Resource and its monitors, for an overview; it has no picture of the monitor, which shows from the second step on ([Monitoring](#monitoring)). *Decided on 2026-11-10, by Max: "selecting a resource will require a search form", and "when you select a resource, you don't need to have the picture of the monitor".*
- <a id="dec-ux-205"></a>**DEC-UX-205** · A Resource has a tab Monitor, after Preview, with every monitor that watches it, Edit on each, and New monitor ([Monitoring](#monitoring)). *Decided on 2026-11-10, by Max: "on the resource itself within the collection, there is a tab saying Monitor. There, you see all the active monitors, and you can also click to edit the monitor or create a new monitor."*
- <a id="dec-ux-206"></a>**DEC-UX-206** · On the strip of a monitor, one day or week is a dot; a stretch of them is the dot on its last tile with a tail back over the others, a line with round ends, lower than the dot: still one value. What it is compared with is the same shape, hollow, shifted back ([Monitoring](#monitoring)). It replaces the dots on a line and the bars of [DEC-UX-195](#dec-ux-195). *Decided on 2026-11-10, by Max: "you have a 'dot' for the day, a 'tail' when you select the window (tail is a line with rounded corner that is less high than the dot)".*
- <a id="dec-ux-207"></a>**DEC-UX-207** · Data Hub makes the right thing easy and the wrong thing hard: the right step is the obvious one, offered where it is taken, to whoever can take it, with why. Every requirement says which behaviour it makes easy and which it makes hard ([General](#general)). *Decided on 2026-11-11, by Max: "Good software is like good governance, making it easy to do the right things and difficult to do the wrong things."*
- <a id="dec-ux-208"></a>**DEC-UX-208** · Concrete leaves the backgrounds of the day, so neither set holds a picture now. A User who chose it gets one of the set at random, as for any background that left it ([DEC-UX-108](#dec-ux-108)). *Decided on 2026-11-11, by Max: "the concrete background, remove it pls".*
- <a id="dec-ux-270"></a>**DEC-UX-270** · Adding an EDA-Probe: the Owner chooses it from the list for the model type, then its page takes the main area, as building a monitor does. On the left its drawing, animated once, step by step, with Replay, and still for whoever asks for reduced motion; on the right what it asks, its fields, filled in from the roles where they can be and chosen where not, its parameters and what it reads, then Add and run ([EDA](#eda), [DEC-ED-13](../requirements/functional/15-eda.md#dec-ed-13)). *Decided on 2026-11-16, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-21: its layout is replaced by [DEC-UX-324](#dec-ux-324); the drawing animated once with Replay stays.*
- <a id="dec-ux-271"></a>**DEC-UX-271** · The first step of a flow through Claude, such as the Owner Journey, says what the User is about to do and why, what they get on the way, the steps ahead, and that everything they write can be changed later except what cannot be undone ([DEC-CL-18](../requirements/functional/17-claude.md#dec-cl-18)). *Decided on 2026-11-17, by Max: "the flow kicks off by presenting to them what and why they are doing this, and that this should always be the first step in the flow … we need to point out that it isn't definitive, so they can always come back and change."*
- <a id="dec-ux-272"></a>**DEC-UX-272** · Every other step of a flow through Claude keeps its overview folded on top: the bar, the step, done and still open; unfolded, every step with what it holds and its mark, an advised step shown as optional ([DEC-CL-18](../requirements/functional/17-claude.md#dec-cl-18)). *Decided on 2026-11-17, by Max: "for each of the steps, we really need to go back to some kind of flow overview … where are we, what do we already have, what's still missing, what's really required, what's unnecessary". Folded, and "optional" for an advised step, were Claude's proposals, which Max agreed to.*
- <a id="dec-ux-273"></a>**DEC-UX-273** · The Learning Cards of a step of a flow through Claude come folded under "How can we help with this step?", a line each with their icon, number and title, unfolding to the core idea, what it brings and "Read the card". It replaces the strip of [DEC-UX-261](#dec-ux-261). *Decided on 2026-11-17, by Max: "the cards are very large and overwhelming … I would do something like: How can we help with this step? Listing the subjects small, which also can be unfolded. Of course, an icon before it."*
- <a id="dec-ux-274"></a>**DEC-UX-274** · Every link to Data Hub from Claude has one form, the mark of Data Hub, "Open … in Data Hub" and the arrow of a link that leaves, and leads to the spot: in a step, the part or tab that the step writes ([DEC-CL-19](../requirements/functional/17-claude.md#dec-cl-19)). *Decided on 2026-11-17, by Max: "Maybe, always for the links, we add an icon to it." Leading to the spot was Claude's proposal, which Max agreed to.*
- <a id="dec-ux-275"></a>**DEC-UX-275** · Where a text about a Resource is written, a quiet line says what it is for: next to the description from dbt, what the Business Description is for; at the pitfalls and the Known Gaps, the test that tells them apart. In the web app above the editor, in Claude in the window of the step ([DEC-RC-26](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#dec-rc-26), [DEC-RC-27](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#dec-rc-27)). *Decided on 2026-11-17, by Max, after a session with business; the line in the web app was Claude's proposal, which Max agreed to.* *Note of 2026-11-18: replaced by [DEC-UX-279](#dec-ux-279): the line is gone, and the ⓘ of the term gives its definition.*
- <a id="dec-ux-276"></a>**DEC-UX-276** · Neither the window of the step Fields nor the Columns tab shows what is in a field, for now: a look at the data of a field gets its own design first ([DEC-RC-28](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#dec-rc-28), [OP-RC-29](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#op-rc-29)). *Decided on 2026-11-17, by Max: "leave the mini-eda out of it all. Think this is a concept which needs way more attention then doing it real quick now." It replaces the proposal of the same day, a look by itself in Claude and on a click in the web app.*
- <a id="dec-ux-277"></a>**DEC-UX-277** · On Webhooks, a recorded delivery whose run is the Current Version of its dbt project offers "Process again" too, not only a failed one, with a line that says what it is for: the run is read again with what Data Hub does now, and nothing else changes ([FR-AR-14](../requirements/functional/01-artifact-registry.md#fr-ar-14), [EC-AR-06](../requirements/functional/01-artifact-registry.md#ec-ar-06)). *Decided on 2026-11-17, by Max, who saw that the column types from BigQuery had not reached a model in prod after the release: "I still got the issue we did the fix for, so not knowing what the data type is", and asked for the fix in the same pull request. The release had said to process the last run again, which Webhooks offered for a failed run only; offering it for the current run was Claude's fix.*
- <a id="dec-ux-278"></a>**DEC-UX-278** · The MCP App of a Lens is its page as it runs, in the blocks of the other MCP Apps: its name and question, its Collection, the Resources it reads with their Level; its Parameters in a bar, each with the value of this run, a date as a date or as a function with the day it gives today, "Default" on a changed one, and "Run" at their end, enabled once a value changed; the grid with the description of each field on the head of its column, and folded under it what each field holds, for a phone; what it read, its Query ID and "Open the Lens in Data Hub". No SQL and no Request: above 1 GB the User chooses values that read less. A Lens that does not run says why on top, its Parameters greyed. On a phone the bar is one column ([DEC-UX-180](#dec-ux-180)). Drawn in [mcp-lens](screens/mcp-lens.html). *Decided on 2026-11-18, by Max ([DEC-CL-21](../requirements/functional/17-claude.md#dec-cl-21)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-279"></a>**DEC-UX-279** · A term of the dictionary that stands on a screen as a heading or a label carries the marker ⓘ, and its pop-out starts with its definition: the name of the term and one or two sentences of what it means, in the language of the User, then the Learning Cards that explain it, as every pop-out ([DEC-UX-57](#dec-ux-57)). It shows also while none of its cards is written, as its definition is always there ([DEC-LR-28](../requirements/functional/16-learning.md#dec-lr-28)). It replaces the quiet line that said what a text is for ([DEC-UX-275](#dec-ux-275)): the definition says it. First on the page of a Resource: Business Description, Known Behaviour, Pitfalls, Known Gaps and Key Fields, also on the Columns tab and while editing. In Claude, the window of a step of the Owner Journey carries the ⓘ after its title, and a tap unfolds under it the definitions of the terms of the step, each with its name: Business Description at the steps Business Description and Fields, Key Field at Key Fields, and at Pitfalls and Known Gaps both Pitfall and Known Gap, as the test is between them; on a phone too ([DEC-UX-180](#dec-ux-180)). Drawn in [resource-overview](screens/resource-overview.html), [resource-columns-edit](screens/resource-columns-edit.html) and [owner-journey](screens/owner-journey.html). *Decided on 2026-11-18, by Max (translated from Dutch): "You can remove this. I would simply always add an i to the terms we take into the dictionary, so that we give an explanation there."; then: "the i on dictionary terms: show the definition first", in the web app and in Claude, and every line that said what a text is for goes.* *Built on 2026-11-18.*
- <a id="dec-ux-280"></a>**DEC-UX-280** · Run in Query never waits for the Pre-flight. While the Pre-flight of what is written has not come back, or could not be read, the button says "Run" and stays on; a click, or Cmd+Enter, checks the query at once and does what the Pre-flight says: up to 100 MB it runs and shows the result; above it the button turns red to "Approve and run" with the focus, a second confirmation; above 1 GB it becomes "Send to the Data Team"; what keeps it from running shows in the bar. A Pre-flight that could not be read says so in the bar, in place of "not checked yet", and Run checks again. The pause of two seconds while typing stays ([DEC-UX-154](#dec-ux-154), [DEC-UX-142](#dec-ux-142)). *Decided on 2026-11-18, by Max: "also the pre-flight timing, which i changed a while ago is a bit an issue, i would like to run my query quicker then the wait i need to do on pre-flight, what i would like, you can always run the query, if no preflight has ran, the run first runs a preflight and requires another confirmation if he is to big if not you just run or show result"*
- <a id="dec-ux-281"></a>**DEC-UX-281** · When the User describes the fields of a Resource with Claude, outside the Owner Journey or at its steps Key Fields and Fields, Claude shows its suggestions in one MCP App, never as text to copy: a row per field, with a box "Key Field", ticked where Claude suggests it or where the field is one already; the name of the field, its type and, quietly under it, its description from dbt; and its Business Description in a box to edit, Claude's suggestion marked "Suggested", a text already on the card marked "On the card", which Claude never replaces, and a text the User changed marked "Edited". A Key Field without a Business Description says it needs one to be published. On top, what Claude based its suggestions on, such as the rows of a Preview it read, and a filter: all fields, the suggested ones, the Key Fields. At the foot, what "Save to Data Hub" saves, as the User's own; only that click writes, with what the card showed as before: a field a colleague described meanwhile keeps its text until the User chooses theirs or the colleague's. Claude is told what was saved, without a message ([DEC-UX-261](#dec-ux-261)). On a phone each row is one column ([DEC-UX-180](#dec-ux-180)). Drawn in [mcp-fields](screens/mcp-fields.html) ([DEC-CL-22](../requirements/functional/17-claude.md#dec-cl-22)). *Asked on 2026-11-18, by Max: "when describing a resource trough the mcp, is it possible to 'ask claude' to do a suggestion on which fields to declare as important and create a description for each field BUT to visualize all this in a card and enable the user to check/uncheck fields as important and to 'edit' the description claude has suggested? I think this is much user friendly instead of typing it"; then, of the choices put to him: one card for both, Key Fields and descriptions; a suggestion only for a field without a description, as a text on the card is kept; and Claude may also read a small Preview of rows. Designed on 2026-11-18, at the request of Max, to review.* *Approved on 2026-11-18, by Max: "go, build it pls". Built on 2026-11-18.*
- <a id="dec-ux-282"></a>**DEC-UX-282** · A User makes a Project, a set of queries they work on, and puts queries in it. Its queries are its flagged queries: while the User works in it, they are the row on top of the screens of Query, and flagging or unflagging a query there adds it to the Project or takes it out. In no Project, the row holds their own flagged queries, with a button "Add to a project" ([Projects](#projects), [DEC-UX-236](#dec-ux-236)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max: "I reguallarly create different queries in 'one project' therefor I would like to be able to create a 'project' folder and attach queries to it. these are then all flagged queries", and "add a button; if no project; add to project if in a project all movement of flagging/unflagging happens in the project". The details are proposed, with [OP-UX-92](#op-ux-92) to [OP-UX-96](#op-ux-96).* *Changed on 2026-11-20: a User no longer works in a Project; the active Project follows what is open ([DEC-UX-317](#dec-ux-317)), and queries go in with the Project button ([DEC-UX-318](#dec-ux-318)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-283"></a>**DEC-UX-283** · The row on top holds more than ten queries: the first ten keep ⌘1 to ⌘0, any further one has no key ([Projects](#projects)). This replaces "up to ten" of [OP-UX-84](#op-ux-84). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max: "the pinned querys should be a list longer then 10". The details are proposed, with [OP-UX-96](#op-ux-96).*
- <a id="dec-ux-284"></a>**DEC-UX-284** · The row on top has no button for the quick window: ⌘⇧P and ⌘K open it ([Projects](#projects), [DEC-UX-237](#dec-ux-237)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max: "remove the cmd-shift-p from the bar".*
- <a id="dec-ux-285"></a>**DEC-UX-285** · The panel of Query shows the Projects of the User above the history, and its search looks through both. A Project has a page with all its queries, where it is renamed, described, shared and deleted ([Projects](#projects)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max: "in the sidebar, above the history, you have an overview of all projects", "search is in both projects as well as in queries", "you can open a project detail containing all the queries" and "you can update, delete rename etc a project". The details are proposed, with [OP-UX-100](#op-ux-100) and [OP-UX-103](#op-ux-103).*
- <a id="dec-ux-286"></a>**DEC-UX-286** · A Project is shared with other Users; a colleague whose Permission group does not let them make or run queries can be added, and Data Hub says so. Everyone who may open a query of a Project has every right on it ([Projects](#projects)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max: "a project can be shared amoung other users (we don't care if they have query rights but we signal it)" and "anybody who has access to the query has full edit privileges". The details are proposed, with [OP-UX-97](#op-ux-97) to [OP-UX-101](#op-ux-101).*
- <a id="dec-ux-287"></a>**DEC-UX-287** · Adding a Resource or a table to a query, or taking a Resource out, changes that query: it reads the new set from its next run, and its history keeps what each run read. Starting a new query with the new set stays possible, in the same sheet, but is no longer the required route ([Query](#query), [DEC-UX-73](#dec-ux-73)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max: "when adding a resource or table to a query, the 'start a new query' isn't the required route any more". The details are proposed, with [OP-UX-102](#op-ux-102).*
