- <a id="dec-ux-288"></a>**DEC-UX-288** · Every command of Projects is in the quick window: on a query, on the page of a Project, and New project on every screen of Query ([Projects](#projects), [Quick window](#quick-window)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max: "also of course, all commands available in cmd-shift-space", read as the quick window ([OP-UX-104](#op-ux-104)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-289"></a>**DEC-UX-289** · The sheet that adds a Resource to a query, or takes one out, ends in two buttons side by side: "Just add", or "Just take it out", which changes this query, and "New query", which starts another with the new set. No choice at its top, and no name or question to fill in: a new query is named after its Resources and the date, and renamed in place ([Query](#query), [DEC-UX-287](#dec-ux-287)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max, on the design of [Projects](#projects): "the add a resource, it should be both buttons 'new query' or 'just add'".*
- <a id="dec-ux-290"></a>**DEC-UX-290** · The page of a Project has a button "New query" that makes an empty query in the Project and opens it: no Resources yet, to add in it, as a query's Resources are changed in it ([Projects](#projects), [DEC-UX-287](#dec-ux-287)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max, on the page of a Project: "could you add; a button new query, empty". The details are proposed, with [OP-UX-105](#op-ux-105).*
- <a id="dec-ux-291"></a>**DEC-UX-291** · Each query on the page of a Project has "Remove from project", always in view; the query stays with whoever made it ([Projects](#projects)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max: "per query the 'remove from project'". The details are proposed, with [OP-UX-106](#op-ux-106).*
- <a id="dec-ux-292"></a>**DEC-UX-292** · The result of a query in Query has Copy as JSON and Copy as CSV in its upper right corner, which copy its first 100 rows with the names of its fields; CSV with a comma ([Query](#query)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max: "add a 'copy result as json' feature to the query result as well as copy a csv", "upper right corner", "limited result; 100 first rows"; of the separators put to him, "Comma". The details are proposed: the types in JSON, the quotes of CSV, the toast and the commands in the quick window.*
- <a id="dec-ux-293"></a>**DEC-UX-293** · The quick window offers New query, which opens the screen to choose its Resources, for who may make queries; New blank query, an empty query in no Project, only for a power user; and New query in each Project the User is in, an empty query in it ([Quick window](#quick-window)). *Decided on 2026-11-19, by Max: "also in the quick-menu, 'new query' and 'new query in project' which opens up a blank new query"; asked whether New query becomes blank for everyone, "provide both, only those with the authority to use tables outside of the datahub need the blank query screen"; of the choices for the Project, "One row per Project".*
- <a id="dec-ux-294"></a>**DEC-UX-294** · The copy of a result is a menu button, a new component: the copy icon with a small caret, named "Copy the result", opens a menu under it with a line on what is copied, Copy as JSON and Copy as CSV, each with a hint; it works by keyboard as a menu does ([Query](#query), [components](components.md#4--containers-and-layers)). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max, on the design of [OP-UX-107](#op-ux-107): "ok voor beide, bouwen maar" ("ok for both, go ahead and build"); he had asked for it "under a 'sub menu' … a button with an icon".*
- <a id="dec-ux-295"></a>**DEC-UX-295** · In the panel of Query, the Project the User works in comes first, with "You work in it" and Close project, which stops working in it; a new query is in no Project unless it is made in one ([Projects](#projects)). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max, on the design of [OP-UX-108](#op-ux-108): "ok voor beide, bouwen maar" ("ok for both, go ahead and build"); he had asked for "'close project' or so" in the left menu, and a new query not made from the Project in no Project.* *Superseded on 2026-11-20: nothing closes a Project; the active one follows what is open ([DEC-UX-317](#dec-ux-317)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-296"></a>**DEC-UX-296** · Reliable since sits on the Known Gaps part of the Overview, in a tile of its own above the gaps, no longer under Known Behaviour; it says its day or "From its first row", the Date Field it is read on, and why not earlier; and it shows the time of the data of the Resource as a small line, before that day shaded ([Detail page of a Resource](#detail-page-of-a-resource), [DEC-RC-29](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#dec-rc-29)). How the tile, its line and its form look is [OP-UX-109](#op-ux-109). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max, on Claude's proposal: "I love your proposal 1, 2, 3, where 3 is a small timeline that is shown. Where it's set, I would say … in its own tile, but on the known gaps page. Indeed, too short. Why not earlier? That's a good thing. I think also you need the option from the beginning of time".* *Note of 2026-11-20: the line first drew the Known Gaps with a period as holes; Max, on the drawing: "Leave the gaps out of this. It will create complexity, which isn't required at that point. Now we're just talking about which is the reference date from where to start reading this data." It draws none.*
- <a id="dec-ux-297"></a>**DEC-UX-297** · The tile of Reliable since, its line and its form are as drawn: the ⓘ on its title; the day large with its Date Field beside it, Why not earlier and who set it; the line from its first row to today, before the day shaded, with a key for the shading and no Known Gaps; Change opens the form in place, with From its first row and From a later day; Not set asks for one of the two, neither chosen, Save off until one is; the Summary reads it first in the part of the Known Gaps ([OP-UX-109](#op-ux-109), [FR-RC-31](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#fr-rc-31)). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max, approving the design as proposed: "Brilliant, this is perfect. You may commit everything and then start building, please."*
- <a id="dec-ux-298"></a>**DEC-UX-298** · The period of a Known Gap is One day or A period, two choices with the dates right behind them: one date, or From until; and under them On the Date Field, optional ([OP-UX-110](#op-ux-110), [FR-RC-33](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#fr-rc-33), [FR-RC-34](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#fr-rc-34)). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max, approving the design as proposed: "Brilliant, this is perfect. You may commit everything and then start building, please."*
- <a id="dec-ux-299"></a>**DEC-UX-299** · A [Flow Box](../definitions.md#flow-box) takes a User through the steps of one flow, in a frame of its own docked on the right of the main area: the page narrows to make room rather than being covered. It is one general component for every flow; describing and publishing a Resource is its first ([FR-MR-20](../requirements/functional/06-maturity-and-release.md#fr-mr-20)). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max, choosing "Push" over a window over the page, and: "It is a general component, and it will be implemented first on this flow."*
- <a id="dec-ux-300"></a>**DEC-UX-300** · A Flow Box stays open on every page of Data Hub until it is closed: going to Query, a Lens or any other page leaves it where it is, and its steps lead back to the page of its object. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "you can jump to other parts of the Data-hub, so if you want to go to queries, it still remains open", "because I've seen in the behavior of colleagues that, for example, they jump to query."* *Note of 2026-11-20, confirmed by Max: "it may remain open on every page of the Data-hub."*
- <a id="dec-ux-301"></a>**DEC-UX-301** · A Flow Box has Minimize, which folds it to a strip on the right edge with its progress, and Maximize on the strip, which opens it again. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "Also add the minimize/maximize button."*
- <a id="dec-ux-302"></a>**DEC-UX-302** · A Flow Box can be closed at any moment, and Publish brings it back ([DEC-MR-20](../requirements/functional/06-maturity-and-release.md#dec-mr-20)). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max, choosing the first of two readings Claude proposed of "it can be switched off": closing it at any moment, with Publish as the way back, rather than a setting that turns the guidance off for good.*
- <a id="dec-ux-303"></a>**DEC-UX-303** · A Flow Box is purely a guide and writes nothing itself: a step leads to its place, opens it for editing and puts its field in focus. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "it's purely a guide, although this guide, when you click on a step, jumps to the proper place, opens up the edit mode, and so on."*
- <a id="dec-ux-304"></a>**DEC-UX-304** · The Flow Box of a Resource holds the steps required to publish, the same list as its Maturity ([DEC-MR-16](../requirements/functional/06-maturity-and-release.md#dec-mr-16)), and tells for each step what it asks, why and how. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "Indeed, the required steps", and, in his description of the flow box, that each step always tells what, why and how.*
- <a id="dec-ux-305"></a>**DEC-UX-305** · The Flow Box looks as drawn in [flow-box](screens/flow-box.html): 340 px wide, its head, the progress in segments, its steps as cards, the step to do now outlined and unfolded with What, Why and How, its Learning Card and its way to its place; minimized a strip of 56 px; and the pop-out of Publish under its button. The What, Why and How of the seven steps are as written there ([OP-UX-111](#op-ux-111)). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "agree".* *Note of 2026-11-20, while building it: Key Fields and Fields are two steps, as on Maturity ([DEC-UX-304](#dec-ux-304)), so the drawing now splits the one it drew as "Key Fields, each described", each with its own What, Why and How; there are seven steps required to publish, then Read back and publish. A step done says what it holds, such as "Written", "`week_start`" or "None, according to An Claes"; who wrote a text and when stays in its History.* *Note of 2026-11-21: a step opens on Why, then What and How ([DEC-UX-334](#dec-ux-334)); the Known Gaps are no step of it any more ([DEC-UX-333](#dec-ux-333)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-306"></a>**DEC-UX-306** · A Flow Box keeps nothing of its own: it reads the state of its Resource each time, and starts from the beginning when it opens, with the first step still open unfolded and a tick beside every step already done. Whether it is open lives only in the open app, and goes with a reload ([OP-UX-112](#op-ux-112)). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "it shouldn't be stored. I think that you just need to look at the resource. What's the state of the resource, and then it should be shown. You don't need to remember on which step you were for a given resource. For me, it may start from the beginning. You open it, and you start. The thing is, if you've already written a business description, then you see a checkbox next to that step."*
- <a id="dec-ux-307"></a>**DEC-UX-307** · In a window narrower than 1360 px, opening a Flow Box folds the panel, which the User opens again as always ([OP-UX-113](#op-ux-113)). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "this is great."*
- <a id="dec-ux-308"></a>**DEC-UX-308** · There is one Flow Box at a time: "Guide me" on another Resource takes the place of the one open ([OP-UX-114](#op-ux-114)). *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "Yes, indeed."*
- <a id="dec-ux-309"></a>**DEC-UX-309** · Editing a Collection starts at its title: for the Owner Unit and the Data Team, a click on its name or description turns both into fields, with Save, Cancel and "Edit more", which opens every field. The head has no Edit button; the quick window offers the same at once. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "'edit' of the resource should only be made available when you click on the title, you get the form and there edit more. Of course this edit is immediately available in the quick menu."*
- <a id="dec-ux-310"></a>**DEC-UX-310** · The head of a Collection has "Messages", as before, and one "New" with a menu: Message, Folder, Resources and Lens, each for who may. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "I'd like 1 button 'new' with underneath 'messages, maps, resources and lens'", and "let the messages as is".* *Message left the menu the same day ([DEC-UX-313](#dec-ux-313)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-311"></a>**DEC-UX-311** · The page of a Collection shows what sits at its top in one table: its folders first, then its Resources and Lenses together, A to Z. No line explains the table, such as which Lenses its Owner Unit added or that new Resources come in at the top. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "1 table containing the lenses, resources and folders", folders first as Claude proposed, and "remove text such as …, as you can see I'm looking to simplify the ui".*
- <a id="dec-ux-312"></a>**DEC-UX-312** · The history of a Collection is no part of its page: the quick window opens it. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "the history just make it available underneath the quick menu".*
- <a id="dec-ux-313"></a>**DEC-UX-313** · "New" on a Collection holds no Message: a Message is written under "Messages", next to it. New holds Folder, Resources and Lens, so a colleague who may make none of them sees no New. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "message may be removed from new, it's just under messages".*
- <a id="dec-ux-314"></a>**DEC-UX-314** · Under Needs attention, each Resource that is not published yet has a line of its own, with a link to it, as every other line there. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "the unpublished resources may be drawn each on screen so also a list".*
- <a id="dec-ux-315"></a>**DEC-UX-315** · The page of a folder is laid out as the page of its Collection: the same "New" on the right of its head (Folder and Lens, with Delete), and what it holds in the same table, with the same columns and without a heading or a line of explanation, such as how the table is ordered or that a row moves with a drag. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: remove "Folders first, then Resources and Lenses, each A to Z." and "Drag a row onto a folder, here, in the panel or in the path above, to move it", "this way it has the same layout as the main screen", and "'In this folder' also removed".*
- <a id="dec-ux-316"></a>**DEC-UX-316** · There are no flagged queries: a query is kept on top of Query by being in a Project. The flags Users set before are taken away; their queries stay where they are, in their history. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "all pinning happens in project so 'free pinning' isn't available anymore", and the flagged queries of today unflagged.*
- <a id="dec-ux-317"></a>**DEC-UX-317** · The active Project follows what is open: a click on a Project makes it active and opens its details; opening a query of a Project makes that Project active; opening a query in no Project, starting a new query that is not made in the Project, or taking the open query out of it, leaves none active. With none active, there is no row on top. No button activates or deactivates a Project, and nothing is kept for it. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "you click on a project, it is active and we open the overview screen", "when you click on a query in the history that is in a project, the project is automatically selected", no row without one, and it is left "when you click on a query outside the project, or a new query not from the project or you deselect the query from the project".*
- <a id="dec-ux-318"></a>**DEC-UX-318** · Every query has a Project button: Add to project in no Project; Change project and Remove from project in one. Add and Change choose a Project, or make a new one. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "if you open a query, you are able to 'add to a project' and then choose a project", and "every query has a button 'project' that enables you to 'add to project', 'change project' and 'remove project' dependant of its state".*
- <a id="dec-ux-332"></a>**DEC-UX-332** · A chart of the EDA never grows beyond the size it is drawn at: on a wide screen it keeps its width and height, so that its words stay the size of the text around it; on a narrow one it shrinks ([EDA](#eda)). *Decided on 2026-11-22, by Max, on the detail of Distribution of numbers on a wide screen: "there should be a maximum width/height, it can't go above 100%".*
- <a id="dec-ux-331"></a>**DEC-UX-331** · The empty tile Add probe stands at half opacity, and in full when it is hovered or focused, so that it does not weigh as much as the cards of the EDA-Probes beside it ([EDA](#eda)). *Decided on 2026-11-22, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-330"></a>**DEC-UX-330** · The drawing of an EDA-Probe names its columns by kind, such as id, date or count, and never by the fields of the Resource, on the card as on the detail: it teaches the idea, and the names of real fields made it too narrow to read ([EDA](#eda), [DEC-UX-268](#dec-ux-268)). Its caption fits inside it. *Decided on 2026-11-22, by Max: "a personalisation isn't required, for me sufficient if you use generic labels such as id, date, count".*
- <a id="dec-ux-329"></a>**DEC-UX-329** · The EDA of a Resource in Claude is an MCP App of its own: its head with the status, the Level and the needed EDA-Probes complete with the bar of the EDA-Setup; how many results read look at this or for you to judge; per EDA-Step each EDA-Probe added with its reading and its result in a sentence, as on its card; the needed ones Coming named once. A run from Claude shows that EDA-Probe, its conclusion and its result per field, with the buttons of the User's reading; what reads more than runs freely is approved in the frame, as a query. The charts stay in Data Hub ([EDA](#eda), [DEC-ED-29](../requirements/functional/15-eda.md#dec-ed-29)). *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-328"></a>**DEC-UX-328** · The list to add an EDA-Probe is a sheet with a search, as Add a resource: it opens at the EDA-Step of the tile, every EDA-Step in its order, each EDA-Probe with its question, Needed, Suggested or On request, and added with its reading, set on part of what it needs, Open, or Coming; filters All, Needed, Data Hub runs it, Added. A click opens its detail ([EDA](#eda), [OP-UX-119](#op-ux-119)). *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-327"></a>**DEC-UX-327** · The guide of an EDA is a second flow in the Flow Box, Set up its EDA: one step per needed EDA-Probe in the order of the EDA-Steps, grouped under them, Coming ones counted; the step to do now is the best next step of the EDA. On the tab Steps of its EDA it is folded to its strip, as that page shows the same; on the detail of an EDA-Probe and everywhere else it is open ([EDA](#eda), [Flow Box](#flow-box)). *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-326"></a>**DEC-UX-326** · The result of an EDA-Probe on several fields has two forms, in the order of the Resource Card: a row per field, with a bar, its numbers and both readings, when its fields need no chart of their own, such as Nulls per Key Field; a block per field, with its own chart, when they do, such as Impossible values. The Owner reads only a result with something to read ([EDA](#eda)). *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-325"></a>**DEC-UX-325** · The charts on the screens of the EDA are illustrations, drawn for the EDA, and do not use semgraph: they teach what a result means as much as they show it, and semgraph is not far enough yet. A chart among them that needs to become a real chart is revisited then ([EDA](#eda)). *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-324"></a>**DEC-UX-324** · The detail of an EDA-Probe opens in the tab Steps of its EDA, in place of its rows, in two columns: on the left, 40%, what it does, with the drawing of its card, smaller, animated once with Replay, why it matters, its parameters and Run, which adds it to the EDA and runs it, the column folding to a strip; on the right, 60%, its result, the conclusion on top, then the result drawn with what is wrong shaded on the chart itself, the numbers, examples and the Owner's reading. The state of the EDA-Setup is not repeated there. For The Grain holds the chart is how many Grain values are on one row, two, three or more ([EDA](#eda), [DEC-UX-325](#dec-ux-325)). It replaces the layout of [DEC-UX-270](#dec-ux-270). *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-323"></a>**DEC-UX-323** · Wherever the fields of a Resource are listed, they keep the order of its Resource Card, unless a clearly stated reason asks for another: a User finds a field where they found it before ([General](#general)). *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-322"></a>**DEC-UX-322** · The tab Steps of an EDA has two columns. On the left, 70%, a row per EDA-Step with its EDA-Probes as cards and, while the EDA-Step has EDA-Probes left, one empty tile of the same size, dotted and grey, with Add probe and what an EDA-Probe is; the EDA-Probes Data Hub suggests move into the list it opens. On the right, 30%, the Resource and the state of its EDA-Setup: the needed EDA-Probes complete, the four places, the best next step, the EDA-Probes on the table as a whole, and per field, in the order of the Resource Card, what covers it, the Key Fields marked. It replaces the summary on top and the suggested cards of [DEC-UX-269](#dec-ux-269) ([EDA](#eda)). *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-321"></a>**DEC-UX-321** · The page of the EDA of a Resource guides its EDA-Setup: its picture is the progress of the setup and the quality of the EDA, and its status, In progress or Explored, stands beside its title. - <a id="dec-ux-320"></a>**DEC-UX-320** · An EDA is started mostly from the Resource itself, on the maturity ladder of its Overview: its Owner sees Start the EDA at 4 · Explored, which creates the EDA, In progress, and opens it in the section EDA; while it is in progress, the ladder shows how many needed EDA-Probes are complete and Open its EDA ([EDA](#eda)). *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max.* *Note of 2026-11-21: Start the EDA is for who holds the Permission Explore Resources (`eda.use`), only Admin to start, rather than for the Owner as such ([DEC-ED-25](../requirements/functional/15-eda.md#dec-ed-25)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-319"></a>**DEC-UX-319** · The row of the active Project starts with a tab for its details, without a key; its queries keep ⌘1 to ⌘0. *Decided on 2026-11-20, by Max: "above there are the tabs of that project and the first tab is a special one, the project detail", the details without a key.*
- <a id="dec-ux-354"></a>**DEC-UX-354** · A User **archives** a Project they no longer work on: it leaves their panel, and is found again in **All projects**, a page of every Project they are in, with In your panel, Archived and All, opened from a quiet line "All projects · n archived" under the Projects in the panel and from the quick window. The word is Archive ("Archiveren", "Gearchiveerd"), not "inactive", as the active Project is the one open ([Projects](#projects), [DEC-UX-317](#dec-ux-317)). It is done from the head of its page, on hover in the panel or in the quick window, at once and without asking, as Back in your panel undoes it. - <a id="dec-ux-355"></a>**DEC-UX-355** · Archiving a Project changes only the panel of who archives it: the others in it keep it in theirs and are not told; its queries work and run as before ([Projects](#projects), [DEC-UX-286](#dec-ux-286)). *Decided on 2026-11-26, by Max, of two ways: for yourself or for everyone in it ([OP-UX-130](#op-ux-130)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-356"></a>**DEC-UX-356** · An archived Project opened again, from All projects, a link or a query of it in the history, stays archived: while it is open it is active, its row on top and in the panel, and its page says you archived it, with "Back in your panel"; once nothing of it is open, it leaves the panel again ([Projects](#projects), [DEC-UX-317](#dec-ux-317)). *Decided on 2026-11-26, by Max, of two ways: it stays archived, or working in it brings it back ([OP-UX-131](#op-ux-131)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-357"></a>**DEC-UX-357** · The Danger zone of a Project is no longer a part of its page: its head ends with two buttons with only an icon, Archive and the bin, and the bin opens its Danger zone in a sheet, Leave it and Delete it with its reason and "Delete for good" in the sheet itself, with on top a line to Archive for who only wants it out of the way; Leave next to one's name opens the same sheet ([Projects](#projects), [DEC-UX-176](#dec-ux-176), [DEC-UX-174](#dec-ux-174)). The Danger zone of a Resource stays a part of its Overview. Drawn in [query-project](screens/query-project.html). *Decided on 2026-11-26, by Max, in Dutch: "the danger zone is still text under the project info; I don't find that graphically ok and would move it behind a button and then a slide-in, for example, a very simple button, only a delete icon or something like that"; built after "build everything first and then you may merge" ([OP-UX-128](#op-ux-128)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-333"></a>**DEC-UX-333** · The Known Gaps part has no "Nothing to add": the Known Gaps are advised, not required to publish, and without a gap the part says none are known. - <a id="dec-ux-334"></a>**DEC-UX-334** · A step of the Flow Box opens on Why it matters, then What it asks and How it is done: the reason first, so that what follows makes sense. *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max: "On the begleid of a publication, I think it's interesting to start with why and then what."*
- <a id="dec-ux-335"></a>**DEC-UX-335** · "Without a Collection" and "To complete" in the panel show how many they hold in a badge before their name, and their line names the role that sees them: "waiting · Data Admins only", "model types to complete · Data Admins only". *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max: "the waiting card, the 10 before the title, the text. data-admin members pls referring to the role".*
- <a id="dec-ux-336"></a>**DEC-UX-336** · Making or editing a Lens lays out its two columns in proportion of the main area, about 70% for the step and 30% for its steps and size check, never in pixels, and the side is never wider than its column; it stacks in one column when the main area is narrow, whatever the window, as the panel and a Flow Box take room too. *Decided on 2026-11-21, by Max: "I think you're not counting percentages in its width … can you work with percentages here?", after the side overflowed the page next to the Flow Box.* *Note of 2026-11-23: a new query follows it too, after Max saw its Resources run out of their card: the list of Resources is never wider than its half, and the two halves stack when the main area is narrow ([A new query](#query)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-337"></a>**DEC-UX-337** · Where the Grain or the mappings of a Resource show, a Data Admin always has the button to change them: in the head of what one row is on the Description, next to "Change model type"; in the head of Entities; and on Columns. - <a id="dec-ux-338"></a>**DEC-UX-338** · Next to every description from dbt, after its tag "From dbt", a quiet "Seems wrong?" opens the Request to the Data Team on the Resource, with the description quoted; while it waits, a line under the description says it is reported, with a link to the Request ([FR-CV-17](../requirements/functional/14-conversations.md#fr-cv-17)). *Decided on 2026-11-25, by Max.* *Raised in #datahub_feedback on 2026-11-25: [the remark](https://immoscoopworkspace.slack.com/archives/C0CM086EZLL/p1795616624057109).*
- <a id="dec-ux-339"></a>**DEC-UX-339** · Every Message has "Delete" among its actions for its writer and for the Owner Unit of its Resource; it asks to confirm in place, naming what goes, the Message or the conversation with its replies ([FR-CV-18](../requirements/functional/14-conversations.md#fr-cv-18)). *Decided on 2026-11-25, by Max.* *Raised in #datahub_feedback on 2026-11-25: [the remark](https://immoscoopworkspace.slack.com/archives/C0CM086EZLL/p1795616625590319).*
- <a id="dec-ux-340"></a>**DEC-UX-340** · Every term of the dictionary that shows on a screen as system text, as a heading, a label, a chip, the name of a step or of an EDA-Probe, a button or a link, is written as a Defined Term, with its capital, and carries the ⓘ of its definition and Learning Cards, in English and in Dutch; a name stays English in Dutch too ([DEC-DH-65](../overview/data-hub.md#dec-dh-65)), as "Grain" in "De Grain klopt". Text that Users write never carries it ([DEC-UX-56](#dec-ux-56)). On a button or a link the text keeps its action and the ⓘ sits right after it, outside it, as a marker of its own: a click on the text does what it always did, a tap on the ⓘ or a rest of a second on the text opens the pop-out, as on every button. Within a sentence, the first time a term stands in it carries the ⓘ; it extends [DEC-UX-279](#dec-ux-279) from the page of a Resource to every screen, and adds Grain and dbt to the dictionary, dbt written lowercase as its makers write it. *Decided on 2026-11-25, by Max (translated from Dutch): "I'm expecting Grain to be capitalised as well as the i info pointing to the learning cards"; "dbt should also be a term"; "Sleutelveld also a term"; and he asked whether it works on fields that are links.* *Look at: [resource-overview](screens/resource-overview.html), [eda](screens/eda.html).*
- <a id="dec-ux-341"></a>**DEC-UX-341** · The button for the Grain of a Resource, for whoever may set it, reads "Edit Grain and relations" ("Grain en relaties bewerken") in all three places: next to "Change model type" in the head of what one row is, in the head of Entities and on Columns; with the graph icon, and the ⓘ of Grain right after it ([DEC-UX-337](#dec-ux-337), [DEC-UX-340](#dec-ux-340)). It replaces "Edit relations". *Decided on 2026-11-25, by Max: "Go", the open point as proposed.*
- <a id="dec-ux-342"></a>**DEC-UX-342** · A description from dbt that seems wrong is reported with "Seems wrong?" - <a id="dec-ux-345"></a>**DEC-UX-345** · A row that is one link or one button as a whole, such as a row of the list of EDA-Probes, a step of the Flow Box or a tab of the Overview, writes its term with its capital and carries no ⓘ; the page or the step it opens explains it, where the term stands as a heading with its ⓘ ([DEC-UX-340](#dec-ux-340)). *Decided on 2026-11-25, by Max: "Go", the open point as proposed.*
- <a id="dec-ux-269"></a>**DEC-UX-269** · The page of the EDA of a Resource shows its health on top: what it covers, drawn on the Resource itself, the four places, what is still needed before 4 · Explored, how the results read, and the best next step. - <a id="dec-ux-267"></a>**DEC-UX-267** · A search finds while it is typed: the last word of the question also counts as the start of a word, without accents and unstemmed, so that "cre" finds Credits and "attributi" finds attribution; the words before it count by their stem, as before, and a last word that is a common word, such as "the", counts as none. - <a id="dec-ux-266"></a>**DEC-UX-266** · In the Columns of a Resource, a field that is one of its Key Fields carries "Key Field" with a key, first among what it serves as, so that a reader sees at a glance which fields matter before reading them all ([DEC-SR-19](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#dec-sr-19)). *Decided on 2026-11-16, by Max: "on a resource sheet, the columns, could you add an indication if the given row is a key-fields pls?"*
- <a id="dec-ux-265"></a>**DEC-UX-265** · The Resources to complete are a card "To complete" in the panel, for the Data Team, under "Without a collection", in the warning colour, each with what it misses; opening one goes to its page, where a warning for the Data Team says what is missing and "Complete it" reopens the steps from the model type on ([FR-CO-45](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#fr-co-45)). *Designed on 2026-11-16, at the request of Max, to review.* *Approved on 2026-11-16, by Max.* *Built on 2026-11-16.*
- <a id="dec-ux-264"></a>**DEC-UX-264** · A field of its model type that is gone shows as a warning on the Resource, for everyone, in the signal colour, with the role, the field and when it went; the Data Team has "Choose another field", which opens Edit relations at the step of the fields. - <a id="dec-ux-263"></a>**DEC-UX-263** · The Description on the Overview ends with what one row is: the model type with its definition, the Grain, and each field of the model type by its role; a Data Admin has "Change model type" in its head, which opens the steps of adding from the model type on, saying what changing drops ([FR-RC-28](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#fr-rc-28)). *Designed on 2026-11-16, at the request of Max, to review.* *Approved on 2026-11-16, by Max.* *Built on 2026-11-16.* *Note of 2026-11-25: the Grain has a button of its own wherever it shows, which keeps the model type ([DEC-UX-337](#dec-ux-337)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-262"></a>**DEC-UX-262** · Adding a Resource is a sheet in four steps: choose a Resource, its model type, its Grain and storage, then its fields and relations. The model type asks for fields, such as when an `event` happened and which field identifies what a `state` is about; they are chosen in the field table, in a column "For the event" (or the state…) after each field, and listed on top of the step, chosen or missing; a required one keeps "Add Resource" greyed until it is chosen ([FR-CO-44](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#fr-co-44)). It replaces the second step of [DEC-UX-58](#dec-ux-58), which held the model type, the storage, the Grain and the mappings together. *Designed on 2026-11-15, at the request of Max, to review.* *Note of 2026-11-15: Max confirmed the fields each model type asks for; [FR-CO-44](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#fr-co-44) is Specified.* *Note of 2026-11-15: Max asked for the Grain and the storage on a step of their own, and for this to be built together with the EDA.* *Approved on 2026-11-16, by Max, with: a `state` also asks when it was created and last updated; every number field of an aggregate is marked; `other` takes "One row per"; no model type is suggested from the words of a Colleciónes model ([DEC-RC-25](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#dec-rc-25)); "Edit relations" and "Change model type" reopen these steps ([DEC-CO-27](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#dec-co-27), [DEC-RC-22](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#dec-rc-22)).* *Built on 2026-11-16.* *Note of 2026-11-16, by Max, in Dutch: for a `state`, when it was created and when it was last updated are not required; only what it is about is ([FR-CO-44](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#fr-co-44)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-261"></a>**DEC-UX-261** · In a flow through Claude, such as the Owner Journey, the window of each step carries its Learning Card as a strip, with its number, title, core idea and gain; the whole card opens as its own MCP App, in five steps, only when the User asks for it ([Claude](#claude), [OP-CL-12](../requirements/functional/17-claude.md#op-cl-12)). *Decided on 2026-11-15, by Max, choosing "Strip per step, full card on ask" from the options Claude proposed.* *Note of 2026-11-17: the strip became a folded line under "How can we help with this step?" ([DEC-UX-273](#dec-ux-273)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-260"></a>**DEC-UX-260** · On a query, the button that opens the Lenses on its Resources says only how many: "2 Lenses", "1 Lens" or "0 Lenses", with "Lenses on these Resources" on a hover; while they are looked up, "Lenses", greyed ([Query](#query), [DEC-UX-22](#dec-ux-22)). The command in the quick window keeps its full name. *Decided on 2026-11-15, by Max: "the button 'lenses on these resources', please shorten it to '2 lenses'".*
- <a id="dec-ux-259"></a>**DEC-UX-259** · On a query, a click on one of its Resources on top of the frame opens what a double-click on its name in the SQL opens: its Summary in a floating window over the query, with "Open the Resource" to go to its page; it no longer goes to the Resource itself. - <a id="dec-ux-256"></a>**DEC-UX-256** · Going to a screen from the quick window puts the cursor in the search of that screen, once it shows, or for a section whose search lives in its panel, in the panel's; not when the screen put the cursor somewhere itself ([Quick window](#quick-window)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch: "when jumping to a window with a search function from the quick window, you may always put the focus on the search (for example jumping to groups)".*

- <a id="dec-ux-254"></a>**DEC-UX-254** · The quick window has, among the commands of Data Hub, Hide the panel or Show the panel on every screen with a panel, and on the screens of Query, Refresh the queries, as their buttons in the head of the panel do ([Quick window](#quick-window)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch: "in the quick menu, with query: refresh queries already in it? - <a id="dec-ux-253"></a>**DEC-UX-253** · With no query flagged, the row of flagged queries is not shown: "Flag this query" in the quick window flags the first, and the row comes with it ([Query](#query), [DEC-UX-236](#dec-ux-236), [DEC-UX-241](#dec-ux-241)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch: "the flag bar for queries can be hidden as long as there are none, that way we save space; you can then add one through the quick menu".* *Note of 2026-11-19: while the User works in a Project, the row shows, even when the Project is empty ([DEC-UX-282](#dec-ux-282)).* *Superseded on 2026-11-20: with no Project active there is no row ([DEC-UX-317](#dec-ux-317)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-252"></a>**DEC-UX-252** · The head of a query has no "New query" button: it is in the head of the panel of Query and in the quick window ([Query](#query)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch: "the new query button can go too, it is already top left in the left menu".*
- <a id="dec-ux-251"></a>**DEC-UX-251** · The name of a query stands on one line above its question, ending in "…" when it is longer; a hover shows it whole ([Query](#query)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch: "the title also always on one line please, with '...' if need be".*
- <a id="dec-ux-250"></a>**DEC-UX-250** · The page of a query started from another no longer says under its question where it started from; its History still begins with it ([Query](#query), [DEC-UX-73](#dec-ux-73)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch, of "Started from …, after run 3": "can go".*
- <a id="dec-ux-249"></a>**DEC-UX-249** · "Reads only" and the Resources of a query stand on one line, which scrolls sideways when they do not fit; "Add a resource" and "Find a table" are small round icons after it, a + and a magnifier, always in view, each with its name on a hover ([Query](#query), [DEC-UX-224](#dec-ux-224)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch: "the list 'reads …' I would like to see on 1 line; 'add a resource' and 'add a table' can be smaller (e.g. only an icon with + or so)", "as you notice, I am trying to make the screen visually lighter". - <a id="dec-ux-248"></a>**DEC-UX-248** · The editor of a query has no "Fold all" and "Format" buttons in its head: Fold all and Format the SQL are commands of the quick window, and Shift+Alt+F still formats; the arrow in the gutter still folds one CTE ([Query](#query), [DEC-UX-239](#dec-ux-239)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch: "the buttons fold all and format can go too, they are in the quick menu now".*
- <a id="dec-ux-247"></a>**DEC-UX-247** · For a power user, a double-click on the name of a table outside Data Hub in the SQL opens a floating window with what Data Hub knows of it: its description, layer and project from dbt; its kind, partitioning, cluster columns, size, rows, partitions and last change from BigQuery at that moment; and its columns with their type from BigQuery and their description from dbt, or from BigQuery where dbt has none; with "Copy the full name" at its foot. - <a id="dec-ux-246"></a>**DEC-UX-246** · The page of a query has no "Hide" button in its head: a User hides a query with the eye on it in the panel, or with "Hide this query" in the quick window, and shows it again the same ways ([Query](#query), [DEC-UX-138](#dec-ux-138)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch: "in query, top right, the hide button can go".*
- <a id="dec-ux-245"></a>**DEC-UX-245** · A sheet that opens to find something, "Add a resource" on a query and "Find a table", puts the focus in its search when it opens ([Sheets](#sheets)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch: "adding a resource and a table: put the focus on the search at once".*
- <a id="dec-ux-244"></a>**DEC-UX-244** · The template "select … from" of the SQL editor becomes "select … from … where true": it ends on `where` and `true` on lines of their own, in the layout of the Data Team, so that every condition after it starts with `and` ([Query](#query), [DEC-UX-141](#dec-ux-141)). *Decided on 2026-11-14, by Max, in Dutch: "query templates: add a 'where true' to 'select'".*

- <a id="dec-ux-241"></a>**DEC-UX-241** · "Flag this query" sits in the row of flagged queries, at its end with the key it will get, and only while there is room, fewer than ten flagged; the head of a query has no Flag, and the × on a tab unflags. - <a id="dec-ux-240"></a>**DEC-UX-240** · Every screen brings its commands to the quick window, as a query does: a command for every button of the screen that does something to what it shows, and for its tabs and parts, under the name of what it shows; what runs, approves, publishes a large Lens or decides a Request puts the focus on its button, where Enter does it ([Quick window](#quick-window), [DEC-UX-239](#dec-ux-239)). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "Please go over all screens and add the commands pls".*
- <a id="dec-ux-239"></a>**DEC-UX-239** · In the quick window on a query, every button of its page that does something to the query has its command: besides those of [DEC-UX-237](#dec-ux-237) and Show info, Run (or Approve and run, or Send to the Data Team), Add the partition filter, Make it a Lens or Update the Lens, Rename this query, Add a Parameter, Remove a Resource, Lenses on these Resources, Show the history or the result, and Copy the Query ID. - <a id="dec-ux-238"></a>**DEC-UX-238** · In the quick window on a query, when the cursor in the SQL is on a word a double-click explains, a field, a Parameter or the name of a Resource, the first command is Show info: it opens what a double-click on that word opens, the descriptions of a field beside it, the window of a Parameter or the Summary of a Resource, and says under its name which word it explains ([Quick window](#quick-window), [DEC-UX-221](#dec-ux-221)). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "the quick tool, when in a query, the cursor on a selectable element (field, parameter or table) the "show info"".*
- <a id="dec-ux-237"></a>**DEC-UX-237** · Cmd+Shift+P opens a quick window anywhere in Data Hub: a search over the commands of the screen it opened on and over every place the User may go to, Resources and the rest. - <a id="dec-ux-236"></a>**DEC-UX-236** · A User flags a query to keep it in a row on top of the screens of Query, and jumps between the flagged queries with Cmd+1 to Cmd+0 (Ctrl on Windows), to work on several side by side ([Query](#query)). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "I want to be able to 'flag' a query. when i do it comes in the 'query-list' which is a lost of quick selectable queries in the screen. the concept is I have multiple queries running side by side and want to jump between those. so the very top of the screen each of these listed with the cmd/ctrl 1 to 0 running over them and to quickly go from one to the other." - <a id="dec-ux-235"></a>**DEC-UX-235** · Typing `count(*` in the SQL editor proposes the template `count(*) as …Count`: it writes `count(*) as rowCount` with `row` selected, so that the User types the name in front of Count, as the Data Team names a count in camelCase ending in Count; Tab leaves it ([Query](#query)). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "'count(*' should propose the template 'count(*) as ??Count' where ?? you fill in yourselve".*
- <a id="dec-ux-234"></a>**DEC-UX-234** · Enter in the SQL editor starts the new line at the indent of the line above, never more or less; Format still lays out the whole SQL ([Query](#query), [DEC-UX-228](#dec-ux-228)). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "when typing in a and you click 'enter' the indention should always be the same as the one above, now it strangly jumps".*
- <a id="dec-ux-233"></a>**DEC-UX-233** · A query opens formatted: the SQL of the User's own query is written in the house layout as it opens, as a click on Format does ([DEC-UX-228](#dec-ux-228)), and kept through its Pre-flight as any change; SQL Format cannot read stays as it is. - <a id="dec-ux-232"></a>**DEC-UX-232** · A greyed "Make it a lens", and "Update the lens" greyed for the same reason, says why on a hover or on keyboard focus, in a pop-out under it: that a Lens is made only on published Resources, then each Resource of the query not published yet with its Level, or each table outside Data Hub it reads, and what makes the button work ([DEC-UX-218](#dec-ux-218), [DEC-LE-22](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#dec-le-22)). Drawn in [query](screens/query.html). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "when greyed out … show a hover indicating this can't be done due to the specific tables whom are not in the proper maturity".*
- <a id="dec-ux-231"></a>**DEC-UX-231** · Edit more is the same four steps as making a Lens, Its fields included: its Owner Unit changes a description after publishing, and a field a new version added, or every field of a Lens made before, takes the description of a field of its Resources of the same name, to save ([Page of a Lens](#page-of-a-lens), [OP-UX-83](#op-ux-83), [DEC-UX-229](#dec-ux-229)). - <a id="dec-ux-230"></a>**DEC-UX-230** · Users of a Lens see the description of each field on the head of its column, on a hover, with a dotted line under its name as on a word the SQL editor explains ([DEC-UX-222](#dec-ux-222)), and under About in "Its fields": each field with its type, its description and where it comes from, a field of its Resources or its author ([Page of a Lens](#page-of-a-lens), [OP-UX-82](#op-ux-82)). Claude gets them with the Lens. Drawn in [lens](screens/lens.html). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "agree on OP-UX-82, build the lens flow".*
- <a id="dec-ux-229"></a>**DEC-UX-229** · Making a Lens is a flow of four steps, one at a time on the left: What it is, Tags, Parameters and Its fields. Drawn in [lens-new](screens/lens-new.html). *Designed on 2026-11-13, at the request of Max: "I would split the screen 'make it a lens' in a 'flow' setup … keep the box on the right, above add the steps by title and it's 'status' (3 tags, 0 parameters, 4 fields, etc)".* *Approved on 2026-11-13, by Max: "build the lens flow".*
- <a id="dec-ux-228"></a>**DEC-UX-228** · Format follows the house layout of the Data Team for dbt, in lower case, as far as a formatter goes, with a small step after sql-formatter for `with`, the date parts and `interval (…)` ([Query](#query), [OP-UX-80](#op-ux-80)). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "agree, lowercase".*
- <a id="dec-ux-227"></a>**DEC-UX-227** · Making a Lens shows each field of its result with a description: a field named as a field of its Resources takes that description; for any other, its author takes the description of a field of its Resources, searched, or writes one. Drawn in [lens-new](screens/lens-new.html). *Designed on 2026-11-13, at the request of Max: "do not over complicate the 'inheritance logic' but enable to 'select a fields description from the source tables' when you can't find the description".* *Approved on 2026-11-13, by Max: "build the lens flow".*
- <a id="dec-ux-226"></a>**DEC-UX-226** · The grid of the result of a query takes all the height of its pane, with no room left under its count and pages, rather than at most 620 px; a Lens and a Preview keep theirs. *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "the height of the query result pane, the table, should always be 100%".*
- <a id="dec-ux-225"></a>**DEC-UX-225** · Resources added to a query, and tables outside Data Hub found by a power user, are picked one or more at once, and come into the SQL as a CTE each, at its start: after `with`, before the CTEs already there, or with a `with` of their own before the first statement, comments on top staying on top. Each CTE is named after its table, with `_2` when that name is taken, selects its fields one a line, and filters on its recent partitions, as the SQL of a new query does; the sheet shows which CTEs come before they do ([Query](#query)). Drawn in [query](screens/query.html) ("Add a resource", and "Find a table" seen as the Data Team). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "the 'add resource' and 'add table' views, should support adding more then 1 resource at the same time" and "when adding resources or tables, the query should be altered by adding the tables and resources as cte's in the beginning of the query"; on the design, "great".*
- <a id="dec-ux-224"></a>**DEC-UX-224** · Next to "Add a resource", a power user has "Find a table": a sheet that searches the tables Data Hub knows outside its Resources, by name, description and layer, unfolds one to its columns, and adds the ones ticked to the SQL as CTEs ([DEC-UX-225](#dec-ux-225)); a Resource found there starts a new query with it ([FR-SR-58](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#fr-sr-58)). Drawn in [query](screens/query.html) (seen as the Data Team, "Find a table"). *Designed on 2026-11-13, at the request of Max: "for the admin, whom can read more tables then only those in the resources, I would like to add a new tab next to 'add a resource', 'find a table' which opens up a slide-in with a search functionality on tables in the bigquery projects". Approved on 2026-11-13, by Max, with [OP-UX-81](#op-ux-81).*
- <a id="dec-ux-223"></a>**DEC-UX-223** · The SQL in the editor is coloured by kind of word, as BigQuery colours it: keywords, functions and types blue, text in quotes green, numbers teal, Parameters magenta, comments grey; the names of tables, CTEs and columns keep the colour of the text. Drawn in [query](screens/query.html). *Designed on 2026-11-13, at the request of Max: "colouring would be great", with a picture of the editor of BigQuery.*
- <a id="dec-ux-222"></a>**DEC-UX-222** · A word of the SQL that a double-click explains, a Parameter, the name of a Resource or a field of the Resources of the query, has a dotted line under it, softer than the word; a hover says what a double-click opens. Drawn in [query](screens/query.html). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max: "underneath the markable words to add a scribble line or dotted line to indicate it's 'more' presence"; dotted, as a wavy line already marks an error.*
- <a id="dec-ux-221"></a>**DEC-UX-221** · A double-click on a word of the SQL of a query selects it and opens what it names: a Parameter its window, the name of a Resource its Summary in a floating window with "Open the Resource", a field its type and descriptions, from the Owner and from dbt, in a pop-out at the word ([Query](#query), [FR-SR-57](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#fr-sr-57)). Drawn in [query](screens/query.html) (the switch "Double-click"). *Designed on 2026-11-13, at the request of Max: a Parameter opens, a Resource opens "a modal with the details of the resource (cfr the "samenvatting" of the resource and a link 'open resource')", a field shows "both dbt as well as business description". Approved on 2026-11-13, by Max: "floating window is fine" ([OP-UX-79](#op-ux-79)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-220"></a>**DEC-UX-220** · A power user, with the Permission "Query tables outside Data Hub", may read in a query every table Data Hub knows that is no Resource, and start a query from SQL without Resources. Drawn in [query](screens/query.html) (the switch "SQL") and [query-new](screens/query-new.html) (seen as the Data Team). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max ([DEC-SR-46](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#dec-sr-46)).*
- <a id="dec-ux-219"></a>**DEC-UX-219** · In the floating window of a Parameter of a query, Add or Save keeps the Parameter and closes the window, as the work in it is done; when the Parameter cannot be kept, such as without a name or a value, the window stays open and says why ([DEC-UX-71](#dec-ux-71)). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-218"></a>**DEC-UX-218** · On a query that reads a Resource not published, "Make it a lens" is greyed, with why: a line under the question names the Resource, with a link to it, and says that a Lens is made only on published Resources ([DEC-LE-22](../requirements/functional/09-lenses.md#dec-le-22)). *Decided on 2026-11-13, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-217"></a>**DEC-UX-217** · The canvas of the Lineage takes all the room left in the frame below the tabs, across its whole width, rather than a height that follows its longest column: the walk opens on the whole screen, without scrolling, and the canvas is never taller than the frame, so that its zoom buttons stay in sight. It never gets lower than 320 px. A zoom or a pan of the User stays until the next step, which brings what was walked into view again ([DEC-UX-152](#dec-ux-152)). *Decided on 2026-11-12, by Max.*
- <a id="dec-ux-216"></a>**DEC-UX-216** · Where a field maps to an Entity, its identity shows in full, the Entity and its identifier, such as `user.userId` or `subject.subjectId`: in Entities, in the Summary and in Columns. Only the labels of the picture in Entities name the Entity alone, for room. An event keeps its identity, such as `homeLoanSimulation.generate`. Screens: [resource-overview](screens/resource-overview.html) (`?ov=entities`), [resource-detail](screens/resource-detail.html). *Decided on 2026-11-12, by Max: "show the full identifier".*
- <a id="dec-ux-215"></a>**DEC-UX-215** · The lines of the Lineage have no arrowhead: a smooth curve from the right of a model to the left of the next. Screen: [resource-lineage](screens/resource-lineage.html). *Decided on 2026-11-12, by Max: "haal de pijlen maar van de lijnen af, heeft weinig toegevoegde waarde".*
- <a id="dec-ux-214"></a>**DEC-UX-214** · The Owner Unit of a Collection removes a Resource from it while it is not published, where a Data Admin does: the button at the end of its row, on the page of the Collection and of its folder, and in its Danger zone. It follows the Permission "Remove Resources from a Collection" ([DEC-PL-08](../requirements/functional/12-permissions-and-library-cards.md#dec-pl-08)). Screens: [collection-folder](screens/collection-folder.html), [resource-overview](screens/resource-overview.html) (`?ov=danger`). *Decided on 2026-11-12, by Max: "an owner of a dataset should be able to remove a resource when he is not published"; on the design, "the designs are okay".*
- <a id="dec-ux-213"></a>**DEC-UX-213** · Admin gets a tool Withdrawn, after Permission groups: every Resource that dbt withdrew, also those without a Collection, which show nowhere else since [DEC-SR-45](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#dec-sr-45), with its Key, dbt project, Collection, Level and when it was withdrawn; a row opens the Resource. Screen: [admin-withdrawn](screens/admin-withdrawn.html). *Decided on 2026-11-12, by Max: "an admin should be able to view somewhere all the resources that have been removed"; the place chosen by Max the same day, a tool of Admin; on the design, "the designs are okay".*
- <a id="dec-ux-212"></a>**DEC-UX-212** · A Data Admin removes a Resource from its Collection wherever it shows in the Collection: at the end of its row on the page of the Collection and, alike, on the page of its folder; and in the Danger zone of the Resource, explained before its button. Screens: [collection-folder](screens/collection-folder.html), [resource-overview](screens/resource-overview.html) (`?ov=danger`). *Decided on 2026-11-12, by Max: on the page of a folder the button was missing, "found a bug"; "you may also add it to the danger zone in the overview of a resource".*
- <a id="dec-ux-211"></a>**DEC-UX-211** · No password manager offers itself on a field of Data Hub: every text field and text area of the web app and of the MCP App carries the marks with which 1Password, LastPass, Bitwarden and Dashlane leave a field alone, as Data Hub asks for no password ([General](#general)). *Decided on 2026-11-12, by Max: "on a lot of fields I see this 1Password suggestion … actually nowhere in the website this is needed".*
- <a id="dec-ux-210"></a>**DEC-UX-210** · Closing a Message opened in context leaves the User on the page of its object, without the window; it no longer goes back to the Message Centre, which stays the link at the foot of the window ([DEC-UX-30](#dec-ux-30), [FR-CV-09](../requirements/functional/14-conversations.md#fr-cv-09)). *Decided on 2026-11-12, by Max: closing it jumped back to the Message Centre, "this is not the required behaviour".*
- <a id="dec-ux-209"></a>**DEC-UX-209** · Profile on My page has a tile Slack, beside Language: the Slack account Data Hub finds for the User, and the way to give another address, confirmed with a code sent to that account in Slack ([My page](#my-page)). *Designed on 2026-11-11, at the request of Max: "the ability to update your slack address (only in env where slack is setup or uat of course)". Approved on 2026-11-11, by Max, with [OP-LN-08](../requirements/functional/07-log-and-notifications.md#op-ln-08) and [OP-UX-78](#op-ux-78).*
- <a id="dec-ux-203"></a>**DEC-UX-203** · Every environment but prod wears a ribbon with its name, teal, across the upper right corner of the window, on every screen of the web app, the sign-in included, and the title of the tab begins with it; prod wears none ([The environment](#the-environment)). A band along the top edge and a tag under the mark in the rail were drawn too. *Decided on 2026-11-10, by Max: "ribbon looks great, you may implement".*
- <a id="dec-ux-202"></a>**DEC-UX-202** · The page Groups lists the groups a User is in or manages, and one opened shows its members, who manages it, what it owns and manages, and its history, with adding a colleague by name or address, taking one out, choosing who manages it, and deleting it once it owns and manages nothing ([Groups](#groups)). *Designed on 2026-11-10, at the request of Max, to review.*

## Open points

Views, the number of colleagues, or use in Lenses. *Look at: [collections-start.html](screens/collections-start.html), the sections "Popular collections" and "Lenses to discover", Popular.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: removed for now ([DEC-UX-86](#dec-ux-86)).*

- [x] <a id="op-ux-03"></a>**OP-UX-03** · **Start screens** for Lenses and Reports. *Note of 2026-10-24: Lenses is designed (`lenses-start`, withdrawn on 2026-10-26, [DEC-UX-33](#dec-ux-33)); Reports remains.* *Note of 2026-10-26: the start screen of Lenses is withdrawn with its section ([DEC-UX-66](#dec-ux-66)); Reports remains.* *Look at: [collections-start.html](screens/collections-start.html), the start screen of Collections, as the pattern; the Reports icon in the rail leads nowhere yet.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: no start screen for Reports; the Reports icon goes and Reports live under Collections ([DEC-UX-89](#dec-ux-89)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-04"></a>**OP-UX-04** · **Tabs** Lineage, EDA, History, Conversations and Preview. *Note of 2026-10-22: Conversations is designed (`resource-conversations`, withdrawn on 2026-10-26); Lineage, EDA, History and Preview remain.* *Note of 2026-10-23: Preview and Queries are designed ([resource-preview](screens/resource-preview.html), [resource-history](screens/resource-history.html)), to review; SQL went to its own section ([DEC-UX-17](#dec-ux-17)).* *Note of 2026-10-26: the tab Conversations is withdrawn ([DEC-UX-67](#dec-ux-67)).* *Look at: [resource-detail.html](screens/resource-detail.html), its tabs; Lineage, EDA and History are not designed; [resource-preview.html](screens/resource-preview.html); [resource-history.html](screens/resource-history.html).* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: no EDA in v1; Lineage walked step by step; History lists the queries that used the Resource ([DEC-UX-90](#dec-ux-90)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-05"></a>**OP-UX-05** · **Collection palette:** align the three additional colours with the chart language. *Decided on 2026-10-25, by Max: the colours come from a configuration file and are tuned after testing on uat ([DEC-UX-60](#dec-ux-60)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-06"></a>**OP-UX-06** · **Screens on mobile** or in narrow windows. *Look at: [collections-start.html](screens/collections-start.html), in a narrow browser window; [resource-detail.html](screens/resource-detail.html), the same.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: not supported; below 900 pixels a single message says so ([DEC-UX-91](#dec-ux-91)).*

Raised while the design was taken into this repository, on 2026-10-22; not in the design session:

- [x] <a id="op-ux-07"></a>**OP-UX-07** · **Icons:** this design takes Phosphor as the one family, with its line icons for the icon of a Collection. [DEC-CO-13](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#dec-co-13) and [DEC-CO-14](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#dec-co-14) take Tabler Icons, and catalogue and the web app already offer every outline icon of Tabler but the logos of brands. Decide which family Data Hub uses. Moving the icons of existing Collections to Phosphor means mapping their names, as the two sets name their icons differently. *Decided on 2026-10-25, by Max: Phosphor, with the light weight favoured and the others possible ([DEC-CO-19](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#dec-co-19)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-08"></a>**OP-UX-08** · **Collection palette:** this design has six colours (magenta, bordeaux, teal, ochre, slate blue, olive). [DEC-CO-07](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#dec-co-07) and the app have the nine of the "muted" palette by Paul Tol, and catalogue stores those names. Decide which palette applies, and how the colours of existing Collections move over. Together with [OP-UX-05](#op-ux-05) and [OP-CO-02](../requirements/functional/03-collections.md#op-co-02). *Decided on 2026-10-25, by Max: one configuration file holds the palette and grows over time; it starts with the six of this design and the nine existing Collections use, and testing on uat shows which stay ([DEC-UX-60](#dec-ux-60)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-09"></a>**OP-UX-09** · **Look:** [DEC-PF-22](../requirements/technical/00-platform.md#dec-pf-22) asks for the colours of the brandbook, with teal on larger areas, and one light look without a dark mode. This design has warm greys, colour only on content, and a light and a dark theme. Decide whether this design replaces those parts of DEC-PF-22. *Look at: [collections-start.html](screens/collections-start.html), with the moon at the foot of the rail for the dark theme; [sign-in.html](screens/sign-in.html).* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: the look of this design ([DEC-UX-104](#dec-ux-104)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-10"></a>**OP-UX-10** · **Stack:** decide what builds this design: React with Mantine, as [DEC-PF-06](../requirements/technical/00-platform.md#dec-pf-06) and DEC-PF-22 decide, or Svelte, as other repositories of Immoscoop use (raised on 2026-10-20). The design itself does not depend on it: its values are CSS variables and its prototypes plain HTML and CSS. *Look at: no screen; a choice for the build, the design holds in either.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: Svelte ([DEC-PF-28](../requirements/technical/00-platform.md#dec-pf-28)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-11"></a>**OP-UX-11** · **Sections:** the navigation holds Collections, Lenses and Reports. The web app today also has Charts, the gallery of the Chart Server, and for the Data Team the search through the dbt projects. Decide where they go. *Note of 2026-10-23: Query joins them as a main section ([DEC-UX-17](#dec-ux-17)).* *Look at: [collections-start.html](screens/collections-start.html), the icons of the rail; [query.html](screens/query.html), Query as a section.* *Note of 2026-10-28: Charts joins the rail, pointing to semgraph ([DEC-UX-89](#dec-ux-89)); the search through the dbt projects for the Data Team remains.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: closed by the rail of [DEC-UX-89](#dec-ux-89); the search through the dbt projects gets no section of its own for now, and models are reached through the Lineage of a Resource ([DEC-UX-90](#dec-ux-90)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-12"></a>**OP-UX-12** · **Capitals in the interface:** the documentation writes Defined Terms with capitals, and so does the interface of the web app today ("New Collection", "Owner Unit"). The prototypes write the interface in sentence case ("New collection", "Your collections", "Owner: Credit team"). Decide which applies to the interface. *Note of 2026-10-25: the language principle writes the kinds of assets as names in lower case inside a sentence ("Wil je deze lens publiek maken?"), which fits sentence case ([NFR-LOC-02](../requirements/non-functional/localisation.md#nfr-loc-02)).* *Look at: [collection-overview.html](screens/collection-overview.html), buttons and labels such as "New lens" and "Owner: Credit team"; [my-page.html](screens/my-page.html).* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: capitals for Defined Terms, also in the interface ([DEC-UX-92](#dec-ux-92)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-13"></a>**OP-UX-13** · **Settings of a User:** the state of the panel and the dismissed intros, per section and with their version, are kept per User in the settings, and the theme is remembered. Data Hub has no settings of a User yet, and no requirement for them; the prototypes keep them in the browser. Decide where they are kept: in the browser, or in Data Hub, so that they follow the User to another device. *Note of 2026-10-25: the rail no longer has a button for settings ([DEC-UX-53](#dec-ux-53)). Where the switches of a User live, such as "reduce motion" and the background ([OP-UX-20](#op-ux-20)), is part of this.* *Note of 2026-10-25: the language of a User is one more such choice ([OP-DH-46](../overview/open-themes.md#op-dh-46)).* *Look at: [my-page.html](screens/my-page.html), the tab Profile; [collections-start.html](screens/collections-start.html), the intro on top, and the panel that stays open or shut.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: in the browser, for now ([DEC-UX-93](#dec-ux-93)).*

Raised on 2026-10-22, with the panel of [DEC-UX-10](#dec-ux-10):

- [x] <a id="op-ux-14"></a>**OP-UX-14** · **A page of a Collection:** clicking a Collection in the panel only unfolds it, as no page of a Collection is designed. Decide whether it also opens a page of the Collection on the right, and what that page shows. Until then, several Collections can be unfolded at once and they fold only when clicked. *Decided on 2026-10-23: clicking a Collection opens its page ([DEC-UX-13](#dec-ux-13)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-15"></a>**OP-UX-15** · **Lenses and Reports in the panel:** they are to join the list of a Collection later, each with the icon of its kind ([DEC-UX-11](#dec-ux-11)). [DEC-DH-64](../overview/data-hub.md#dec-dh-64) has a Collection group Resources only. Decide whether a Collection holds Lenses and Reports too, and if so, whether its list is one mixed list or a small group per kind. Proposal: a group per kind, as a long mixed list is hard to scan. *Note of 2026-10-23: the page of a Collection lists the Lenses built on its Resources ([DEC-UX-14](#dec-ux-14)); the panel does not yet.* *Decided on 2026-10-26, by Max: the panel lists the Lenses of a Collection too, as a group of their own after the Resources ([DEC-UX-65](#dec-ux-65)).*
Raised on 2026-10-23:

- [x] <a id="op-ux-16"></a>**OP-UX-16** · **The icon of a Collection:** the tinted circle behind it is busy (Max, 2026-10-23). An exploration page showed six treatments side by side, in the panel and in the head of the page: A today, B only the icon, C a grey icon with a colour dot, D a thin ring, E the colour on the edge of the card, F a neutral tile. Choose one; it then moves into `base.css` and the screens. *Decided on 2026-10-23: E, the colour on the edge, for real Collections only ([DEC-UX-15](#dec-ux-15)); the exploration page is removed.*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-17"></a>**OP-UX-17** · **A strong colour for "Without a collection":** Max asked on 2026-10-23 for red or another strong colour, as it is for the Data Team only and needs solving quickly. The signal colour today is ochre, which is also a colour of the Collection palette. Proposal: a new token for what needs the Data Team now, a warm red (`#C4432F`, in dark `#E2725E`), with the number waiting in a small red badge; the exploration page showed it. Decide the colour, and whether it becomes a rule beyond this list. *Decided on 2026-10-23: the red, for "Without a collection" only, without a colour bar as it is no Collection ([DEC-UX-16](#dec-ux-16)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-18"></a>**OP-UX-18** · **The panel of Lenses:** the section Lenses has no panel yet. Proposal, as [designed](screens/lens.html): the Lenses the User may use, grouped by Collection in the same tree as the panel of Collections, each with the icon of a Lens and its Level. Decide whether this is it, and how it relates to Lenses in the panel of Collections ([OP-UX-15](#op-ux-15)). *Raised on 2026-10-23.* *Note of 2026-10-24: the start screen of Lenses has the same panel (`lenses-start`, withdrawn on 2026-10-26).* *Decided on 2026-10-26, by Max: there is no section Lenses and no panel of Lenses; Lenses are listed in their Collection ([DEC-UX-66](#dec-ux-66)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-19"></a>**OP-UX-19** · **The Conversations tab of a Resource:** the slide-in shows the Messages on every object, a Resource among them ([DEC-UX-29](#dec-ux-29)). Decide whether the tab Conversations of a Resource goes, so that all objects work the same, or stays next to the slide-in. Proposal: it goes. *Raised on 2026-10-23.* *Decided on 2026-10-26, by Max: it goes, as "Messages" in the header holds them ([DEC-UX-67](#dec-ux-67)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-20"></a>**OP-UX-20** · **The background of a User:** a picture can take the place of the gradient ([DEC-UX-34](#dec-ux-34)), from the sets of [DEC-UX-36](#dec-ux-36), each photo with its painting ([DEC-UX-37](#dec-ux-37)). Decide who chooses it, each User in their settings ([OP-UX-13](#op-ux-13)) or one for everyone; whether a User can add a picture of their own; which background is the default; how signing in picks its photo, such as a new one each day; and whether the app then shows the painting of the photo the User saw when signing in. *Raised on 2026-10-24.* *Note of 2026-10-27: the flowing gradient is one more background to choose from ([DEC-UX-80](#dec-ux-80)).* *Look at: [sign-in.html](screens/sign-in.html); [collections-start.html](screens/collections-start.html), with `?bg=ski`, `?bg=alpenglow` or `?bg=flow` in the address.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: a set of backgrounds, one picked at random at the start and at every switch of the theme ([DEC-UX-94](#dec-ux-94)).*

Raised on 2026-10-24, while designing Learning:

- [x] <a id="op-ux-21"></a>**OP-UX-21** · **A fifth main item:** the rail holds three to four main items, and with Learning it holds five (Collections, Query, Lenses, Reports, Learning). Decide whether Learning is a main item, or is reached elsewhere, for example from the menu of the account or a help button. The design puts it in the rail ([DEC-UX-38](#dec-ux-38)). *Note of 2026-10-25: the principles want no learning-plan landing page as a mandatory starting point ([principles, 3.1](https://immoscoop.atlassian.net/wiki/spaces/DATA/pages/1381138494)). The start screen is not one: every card also opens from context.* *Decided on 2026-10-25: the learning centre leaves the main items, and sits at the bottom of the rail next to the account ([DEC-UX-52](#dec-ux-52)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-22"></a>**OP-UX-22** · **The marker of what the learning centre explains** ([DEC-UX-54](#dec-ux-54)): the design uses ⓘ (U+24D8, circled small i), which reads as "there is an explanation here". Poppins has no such character, so it comes from a symbol font whose look differs a little per system. The alternatives are † (U+2020, dagger), which Poppins has and which reads as a footnote, and ※ (U+203B, reference mark), which is less known here. [learning-hover](screens/learning-hover.html) shows the three in place. Decide the character. *Raised on 2026-10-25.* *Decided on 2026-10-25 by Max: ⓘ (U+24D8), from a symbol font.*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-23"></a>**OP-UX-23** · **Dutch prototypes:** decide how the prototypes follow the language principle ([DEC-DH-65](../overview/data-hub.md#dec-dh-65)). Proposal: a translation table in the prototypes, a key per sentence with its Dutch and English text, Dutch by default and a switch to English, so that [NFR-LOC-02](../requirements/non-functional/localisation.md#nfr-loc-02) shows at work and a name in the table stands out. The alternative is translating the texts in place, which is faster and keeps no English. Either way names stay untouched, and the markers on names ([DEC-UX-59](#dec-ux-59)) come in the same pass. *Raised on 2026-10-25.* *Look at: [my-page.html](screens/my-page.html), the language in the tab Profile; every screen is in English today.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: not needed; the prototypes stay in English. The language principle applies to the app that is built, not to the HTML prototypes.*

Raised on 2026-10-26, in the review of Max:

- [x] <a id="op-ux-24"></a>**OP-UX-24** · **Overriding a description from dbt, and reviewing it** ([FR-RC-16](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#fr-rc-16), [FR-RC-17](../requirements/functional/05-resource-card.md#fr-rc-17)): editing in place ([DEC-UX-09](#dec-ux-09)) covers the Overview only, and no screen yet overrides the description of a field or reviews a text after a Change in dbt. Proposal: on the Columns tab, an action per field, "Override the description from dbt", that opens a field with the description from dbt under it; on the Overview, the definition takes the same action. The review is a sheet over the page, reached from the Message, with the description from dbt before and after the Change, the text of the Owner, and three choices: keep, change, or remove it. *Raised on 2026-10-26, in the review of Max.* *Decided on 2026-10-26: Max chose two descriptions side by side instead of an override, and a Message without a review; designed in resource-detail, resource-edit, resource-columns-edit and messages, to review ([DEC-UX-61](#dec-ux-61)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-25"></a>**OP-UX-25** · **A deprecated Resource** ([FR-SR-50](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#fr-sr-50), [OP-SR-16](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#op-sr-16)): design how the mark shows on the page of the Resource, in the panel, in search and on the Lenses built on it, and where the Owner and the Data Team set it. Proposal: a label "Deprecated" next to the Level, with the reason on hover and on the page; set from the header of the Resource. *Raised on 2026-10-26, in the review of Max.* *Look at: [resource-detail.html](screens/resource-detail.html), the status row, where the mark would show; [collections-start.html](screens/collections-start.html), the panel.* *Note of 2026-10-28: designed as proposed, for Max to judge: the chip "Deprecated" in the status row with its reason on hover, a line under it with who, why and what to use instead, "Deprecate" and "Take it away" in the header for the Owner and the Data Team, and a tag in the panel. Look at: [resource-detail.html](screens/resource-detail.html) with `?dep=on`.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: as designed ([DEC-UX-101](#dec-ux-101)).*

Raised on 2026-10-27, with a query bounded by its Resources ([DEC-UX-73](#dec-ux-73), [DEC-UX-74](#dec-ux-74)):

- [x] <a id="op-ux-26"></a>**OP-UX-26** · **The colour of an error in a query:** a table outside its Resources and an `@name` that is not a Parameter keep it from running. The design shows both in the warning colour, as the signal for a state that needs action; the urgent red is kept for what waits for the Data Team and for a run above the approval threshold. Decide whether an error that blocks a run takes the warning colour, the red, or a colour of its own. *Raised on 2026-10-27.* *Look at: [query.html](screens/query.html), `@leadChannel` in the SQL, which is not a Parameter yet, and Run under it.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: the colours stay as shown ([DEC-UX-97](#dec-ux-97)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-27"></a>**OP-UX-27** · **How long the result of a run is kept,** for its history to show it ([FR-SR-51](../requirements/functional/04-stacks-and-resources.md#fr-sr-51)). The design shows "Show its result" on a recent run and "Its result is no longer kept" on an older one. Proposal: as long as BigQuery keeps the result of the job, about a day; after that the run shows its SQL and values, and can run again. *Raised on 2026-10-27.* *Look at: [query.html](screens/query.html), with `?rtab=history`, and with `?hprev=p13` for the Preview of run 13.* *Note of 2026-10-28: the result of an earlier run now shows only in its Preview ([DEC-UX-88](#dec-ux-88)).* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: as long as BigQuery keeps it; after that, run it again ([DEC-UX-99](#dec-ux-99)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-28"></a>**OP-UX-28** · **Back to an earlier run:** "Back to this SQL and values" takes the SQL and the values of an earlier run into the query, and its next run is a new variation. Decide whether that is the way back, or whether an earlier run opens on its own, read only, to copy from. *Raised on 2026-10-27.* *Look at: [query.html](screens/query.html), with `?rtab=history`: "Back to this SQL and values" on an earlier run.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: both, as Preview, read only, and Apply ([DEC-UX-88](#dec-ux-88)).*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-29"></a>**OP-UX-29** · **The name of a query started from another:** the name is suggested from its Resources and the date only ([DEC-UX-23](#dec-ux-23)), which gives long names, such as "Weekly lead volume, lead sources and partner contracts, 27 Oct". Decide whether it may be suggested from the query it started from and the Resource added, such as "Leads per source name + partner contracts". *Raised on 2026-10-27.* *Look at: [query-new.html](screens/query-new.html), the suggested Name at the top.* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: kept as it is for now, the name from its Resources and the date ([DEC-UX-23](#dec-ux-23)); to iterate later.*
- [x] <a id="op-ux-30"></a>**OP-UX-30** · **Removing a Resource as a new query:** decided to try ([DEC-UX-73](#dec-ux-73)), as a query on fewer Resources has a very different list of Lenses. Review after testing whether it is too heavy in use, for instance when the SQL still reads the Resource and the new query starts with an error. *Raised on 2026-10-27, by Max.* *Look at: [query.html](screens/query.html), the × on a Resource in the scope above the SQL, which opens the sheet for a new query; [query-new.html](screens/query-new.html).* *Decided on 2026-10-28, by Max: the screen is right as it is; removing a Resource stays a new query ([DEC-UX-73](#dec-ux-73)).*

Proposal: the Permission group says what a User may do; their Owner Units, still Workspace groups ([DEC-UM-04](../requirements/functional/02-user-management.md#dec-um-04)), say on which Collections, so that "Describe and publish Resources" can reach only those of their Owner Units.
